| **Min speed** | 53 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261003_141057.csv`, `tools/finalize.py`); TORCS time **73.820 s** → on screen 1:13:82 (v0.91 73.974: −0.154 s; largest section changes 700 m −0.110, 3,300 m −0.022, 800 m −0.022, 200 m +0.022, 2,500 m +0.020, 400 m +0.020). Damage 0, top 284 km/h, slowest corner 53 km/h at ~3,273 m (hairpin). Max \|trackPos\| 0.693 at ~2,473 m (flick; v0.91 0.679), hairpin 0.57; slides: flick 14.5 km/h (exit 12.1), kink 15.2, hairpin exit 8.1, elsewhere 24.3 at ~3,272 m (the hairpin's rotation in 1st, as v0.90/v0.91: 24.0). **Suites:** 73.945 / 74.028 / 73.920, **all-30 73.964** (v0.91 74.086; per suite −0.121 / −0.108 / −0.134), max 0.851 / 0.796 / 0.725 (v0.91 0.851 / 0.784 / 0.728), **0 of 30 off**; the 0.851 is again the `turn_grip_aero` + run on the flick's left arc (~2,461 m). **Shifted bases: 0 of 40 off** (`brake_margin` 14: 74.058 / 74.072, max 0.868 / 0.918, the 0.918 being the run with a further −2 at the hairpin exit, ~3,277 m; `line_offset` 0.5: 74.056 / 74.083, max 0.782 / 0.794); their mean is 74.067 against v0.91's 74.098. **How sure:** over all 70 runs (30 + 40) v0.92 is 74.023 against 74.093 (−0.070). Every variant that keeps the pedal above 0.01 through the shift is 0.04–0.09 s faster than v0.91 on the 30 runs (18–27 of 30 runs faster) and 74.02–74.06 over the 70; the chosen one is the best of about 14 neighbours, so part of its −0.122 is a favourable draw (`shift_steps` 2 / 4 on the committed code: 74.045 / 74.020). A gain of 0.05–0.07 s is well supported (all three suites and the shifted bases' mean agree in sign for every variant); the extra 0.05 s is not. **Step trace** (3rd → 4th at 126 m): speed per step +0.7 / +0.6 / +0.6 km/h through the shift (v0.91: +0.1 / +0.3 / +0.6), 1.1 km/h ahead six steps later; the engine reading stays at 19,341 / 19,188 / 19,065 rpm for the three steps and then comes down along the slip law (18,396, 17,925 …) instead of dropping to 15,788 at once; the rear wheels no longer dip (rear over-speed −4.5 m/s instead of −15.0 on the shift step). 4th → 5th: the engine reading climbs 19,482 → 19,579 during the hold, the limiter's reading (18,700 true ≈ 19,580 read), on the third step: the reason 4 or more steps are no better. The 1st → 2nd upshifts at full lock (flick 2,472 m and 2,499 m, hairpin 3,271 m; throttle 0.08–0.4) are unchanged in speed and slide (hairpin 57.5 → 52.9 km/h over the six steps after the shift, v0.91 58.1 → 53.6; hairpin slide 24.3 km/h, exit 8.1); the launch is unchanged (0–200 m 4.526 s on both laps). The straight sections do not show the gain at the telemetry's 0.02 s resolution; on this lap the difference is in the bends (700 m −0.110 s: the 786 m bend, slowest point −0.05 → −0.16 of the inside, the reverse of v0.91's loss there). At 159–200 m (start straight, 220 km/h) the plan brakes twice (allowed 216–242 km/h; 38 steps off full throttle, v0.91 30), the car is 6 km/h slower at 200 m and the 4th → 5th upshift comes 14 m later: a false-braking place not yet looked at. **Patterns** (`tools/patterns.py`, v0.91 → v0.92): steering reversals 45 → 44, episodes 1 → 0 (0.3 → 0.0 s; the 536–546 m set-up flip did not occur on this lap); target flips 105 → 105; plan jumps > 20 km/h 106 → 94; brake applications 39 → 39 (short touches 22 → 21, re-applied within 10 steps 14 → 13); shifts 47 → 45, undone within 1 s 3 → 4 (the three full-lock 1-2-1 / 2-1-2 stints as before, plus 4-5-4 at 373 m: the later 4th → 5th upshift is followed 0.93 s later by the braking downshift for the 450 m bend at pedal 0.70; not a hunt); TC cut time 11.8 → 11.9 s. Nothing worse. **Width** (`tools/width.py`, entry −80 m / slowest point / exit +80 m): 448 m +0.21/+0.14/+0.33 (was +0.22/+0.15/+0.30); 776 m +0.20/−0.16/+0.31 (was +0.20/−0.05/+0.32; 116 → 119 km/h, exit 179 → 184); 1,045 m +0.36/−0.48/+0.19 (within 0.01); 1,528 m +0.09/−0.43/−0.10 (was +0.10/−0.38/−0.13; 138 → 139 km/h); 1,928 m +0.30/−0.19/+0.08 (slowest point was −0.17); flick −0.28/+0.69/−0.08 (was −0.29/+0.67/−0.08); the 2,674 m row is gone (not a separate slowest point on this lap); 2,985 m +0.14/−0.46/+0.03 and the hairpin (tool: R −0.22/−0.52/−0.38) within 0.02. The line did not change by design; the differences are lap-to-lap. **Follow-ups measured on top** (all-30, base 73.964; all 0 of 30 off): `shift_steps` 2 / 4 → 74.045 / 74.020; `clutch_top` 17,500 → 74.007 (max 0.837); `upshift_rpm` 18,400 / 18,800 → 74.027 / 74.573; `thr_zero` 1.3 → 74.007 (max 0.812); `corner_speed` 79 → 74.085 (max 0.834); `brake_load_fast` 0.75 → 74.039 (max 0.939). None gains. |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — all-30 74.086 → 73.964 (−0.122 s; about 0.05–0.07 s of it well supported), lap 1:13.97 → 1:13.82, 0 damage, 0 of 30 off with the suite max unchanged at 0.851, shifted bases 0 of 40 off (on-track max 0.919 → 0.918): a gain that spends no margin. |
| **Learned** | The simulator punishes any gear change made with the clutch pedal below 0.01: it opens the clutch itself and caps the throttle at 0.1 for the shift time. The driver's slip law already kept the pedal up on the steps after an upshift, so only the shift step itself was lost; any pedal above 0.01 there recovers it (0.04–0.07 s a lap over 21 upshifts). How the engine is brought down afterwards matters a little too: held at `clutch_slip` for the shift time it stays near 18,500 rpm and the rear wheels are not jerked (over-speed dip −15 → −4.5 m/s), the best of the variants, though the suites cannot separate it firmly from its neighbours. The limit of the idea is the limiter: in 5th the engine reaches 18,700 on the third held step, and a longer hold or a later upshift (`upshift_rpm` 18,800: +0.61 s) runs into it. The same rule on downshifts (pedal kept above 0.01 so the engine braking is not interrupted) gains nothing and costs flick margin (max 0.941). On top of this the neighbouring drivetrain and margin knobs are all flat or slower (`clutch_top`, `clutch_k`, `upshift_rpm`, `thr_zero` 1.3, `corner_speed` 79, `brake_load_fast` 0.75). New and open: the plan brakes on the start straight at 159–200 m at 220 km/h (two brake touches, 30–38 steps off full throttle, ~6 km/h at 200 m carried to the 450 m braking point). On the suites' resolution: per-run differences between two versions have a spread of 0.10–0.20 s, so a mean difference over 30 runs is good to about ±0.03 s, and picking the best of many neighbours overstates it. |

## v0.93 — Full brake pedal beyond the lift band (`lift_pct` 1.0 → 3.5)

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.93 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.93`). One mechanism in the brake block: the **lift band no longer comes off the brake pedal**. Until v0.92 the pedal was `(speed − allowed − band)·brake_gain`; now it is `(speed − allowed)·brake_gain` once the car is beyond the band (line 900; was line 891), and the band is wider: `lift_pct` **1.0 → 3.5** % of speed (line 605; still faded in from `lift_v0` 86 over `lift_vw` 57 km/h, both unchanged). Inside the band the car only lifts, as before. New comment lines 887–895 (9 lines; everything from old line 887 on moves down 9). No other value changed. |
| **Why** | Own analysis first, of the v0.92 run's step trace on the start straight (the plan's item, "false plan braking at 159–200 m"). It is two events at a flat-out left kink, neither of them crest load or the slip reference: **(1) 158–165 m**: the plan's allowed speed is the braking distance along the −7° beam (80 → 78 m to the outside edge: 220 → 217 km/h) because the −12° beam still hits the inside edge at 37 m; one lift, two 0.15 brake touches; when the −12° beam clears the inside edge (37 → 107 m, step 291) the bearing jumps −1.5° → −3.6°, the bend is detected and the steering goes 0.00 → 0.38 in two steps, which yaws the car 7° (sideways speed 8.9 km/h) and puts the −12° beam back on the inside edge (allowed 241 → 216). **(2) 180–184 m**: the −12° beam reads 139–156 m but the sharpness plan's leave-the-track check throws it out, because the arc onto it passes the −7° bearing at 81.9 m and that beam is 79 m (2–11 m short); allowed 292 → 218 km/h, brake 0.16 (just over `touch_brake`, so the stored throttle is zeroed), 16 steps back to full throttle. The car leaves the kink at 222.6 km/h at 213 m, the speed it had at 158 m. **Candidates tried (all-30 mean, paired per-run difference to v0.92, n of 30 faster; base 73.964):** (a) *tightened arc* (own, new): instead of discarding a beam whose arc leaves the track at an inner beam, take the tightest inner-beam radius and the shorter chord; it removes event (2) (233 km/h at 209 m instead of 221, 0.067 s gained by 400 m on the single lap) but loses it again from the 450 m braking on: 73.991, +0.026 (14 of 30), tolerance 0.9 / 0.7 of the radius 74.098 / 73.983, gated to smoothed \|steer\| < 0.1 / 0.2 / 0.3 74.097 / 74.013 / 73.998, gated to > 150 / 200 km/h 73.994 / 74.003; an illegal upper bound (allowed speed forced open at 140–215 m, position-gated, diagnostic only) ran 73.800 on the single lap (v0.92 73.820) and braked at ~215 m anyway. **So the start-straight braking is real but not worth lap time on this car: the speed gained is lost again from the 450 m braking on** (inherited estimate of 0.03–0.05 s gained challenged: measured +0.03 s slower to no change). (b) `touch_brake` 0.2 / 0.25: 73.975 / 73.967 (+0.011 / +0.002). (c) a lift band that keeps growing with speed (band factor capped at 1.5 / 2 / 3 instead of 1, so only above 143 km/h): 73.985 / 74.020 / 73.973, suite max 0.908–0.927: the high-speed lifts are not where time is. (d) `lift_pct` alone, pedal still reduced by the band: 1.25 / 1.5 / 1.75 / 2.0 / 2.5 / 3.0 / 3.5 → 73.962 / 73.917 / 73.952 / 73.917 / 73.850 / 73.833 / 73.780, but it spends the margin: suite max 0.954 at 2.5, 1 of 30 off at 3.5 (flick, 2,477 m), shifted bases 1 / 2 / 4 of 40 off at 2.5 / 3.0 / 3.5 (flick ~2,483 m and hairpin exit ~3,293 m); with `lift_v0` 110 (2.5) or `lift_vw` 80 (3.0): 3 of 40 / 1 of 30 + 1 of 40 off. (e) **the chosen form**, the same wider band with the full excess on the pedal: `lift_pct` 2.5 / 3.0 / 3.5 / 4.0 / 4.5 / 5.0 → 74.024 / 73.990 / **73.908** / 73.923 / 73.831 (1 off) / 73.878 (1 off, both `turn_grip_aero` + at the flick, ~2,481 m); 30 % / 50 % of the band still taken off the pedal (at 3.5 / 3.0): 73.894 / 73.885, shifted on-track max 0.957 / 0.964. 3.5 with nothing taken off is the fastest setting that also *gains* margin on both checks. |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (`tools/suite.py` on the final code): all-30 73.908 (−0.056 s paired, 19 of 30 faster), 0 of 30 off, max 0.725 / 0.802 / 0.703; shifted bases 0 of 40 off, on-track max 0.876. The unperturbed lap is not expected to improve (single laps scatter by ±0.2 s). |
| **Lap time** | 1:13.88 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 284 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 56 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261003_151304.csv`, `tools/finalize.py`); TORCS time **73.880 s** → on screen 1:13:88 (v0.92 73.820: **+0.060 s on the unperturbed lap**; largest section changes 700 m +0.044, 2,500 m +0.040, 1,500 m −0.024, 3,300 m +0.022, 1,100 m −0.022, 800 m +0.022). The start straight is unchanged (218.9 km/h at 200 m, 218.8 before). |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — all-30 73.964 → 73.908 (paired −0.056 s, 19 of 30 faster; the noise on a paired 30-run mean is about ±0.03 s, so about 0.03 s of it is supported), 0 damage, 0 of 30 off, and it **gains margin**: suite max 0.851 → 0.802, shifted bases 0 of 40 off with the on-track max 0.918 → 0.876. The unperturbed lap is 0.06 s slower (1:13.82 → 1:13.88), so the best single lap stays v0.92's. |
| **Learned** | (1) Taking the lift band off the brake pedal made every braking zone run the band's width above the plan (0.11 of pedal at 220 km/h with a 1 % band); that is why a wider band always looked like spending margin (v0.88: `lift_pct` 1.4 max 0.980). With the full excess on the pedal the band and the braking zones are separate things: braking follows the plan itself (flick and hairpin margins improve) and the band can be 3.5 %, so a car riding the plan in a bend lifts instead of braking (166 fewer brake steps a lap). The cliff is 4.5 % (flick, `turn_grip_aero` +). The settings scatter (2.5 / 3.0 slower by 0.06 / 0.03 s, 3.5 / 4.0 faster by 0.06 / 0.04 s), so the gain is small and only its sign and the margin are firm. (2) **The start-straight braking has a located cause and no lap-time value by itself**: (i) a sight-limited plan on the −7° beam before the −12° beam clears the inside edge, with the bend detection's steer step (0.38 in two steps at 220 km/h, 7° of yaw) closing that beam again, and (ii) the leave-the-track check discarding a 139–156 m beam for a 2–11 m shortfall at the neighbouring beam. Removing (ii) with a tightened arc gives +10 km/h at 210 m and 0.067 s by 400 m, and the suites give it all back from the 450 m braking on (+0.03 s): the 450 m braking and turn-in cannot use a faster arrival (where exactly it is lost was not traced). Worth re-testing the tightened arc once the turn-in is fixed (v0.94–v0.95). (3) A band growing with speed is not faster: the lift band's value is in the medium bends (100–145 km/h), not at speed. (4) The unperturbed lap and the 30-run mean moved in opposite directions (+0.06 / −0.056 s): the 786 m apex (−0.16 → −0.04 on this lap) decides single laps by that much. |

## v0.94 — Steering cap at speed (`steer_cap` 0.62 above 105 km/h)

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.94 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.94`). One mechanism in the steering block: **\|steer\| is capped at speed**, just before the steering rate limit (lines 767–768, comment 758–766): cap = 1 − (1 − `steer_cap`)·clip((speed − `steer_cap_v0`)/`steer_cap_vw`, 0, 1). New knobs `steer_cap` **0.62** (line 574), `steer_cap_v0` **95** km/h (line 575), `steer_cap_vw` **10** km/h (line 576): no cap up to 95 km/h (hairpin 56 km/h and the flick's arcs 62–85 km/h keep full lock), 0.62 from 105 km/h. Line mapping from v0.93: lines 1–573 unchanged; old 574–754 move +3; old ≥ 755 move +14. No other value changed. |
| **Why** | Own analysis first, of the v0.93 step traces at every bend detection (the plan's item: the turn-in of the 450 m / 786 m bends). **What the traces show:** (1) at 392 m (160 km/h, brake 0.43–0.85) the bearing jumps −2.0° → −3.8°, the line target goes 0 → +0.92 and the steer 0.01 → 0.21 → 0.41 → 0.61 → 0.63 (rate-limited), the car yaws 12.3° (angle −0.215 at 406 m), the heading term takes the steer back to −0.07, and the car reaches the inside (+0.16) already at 416–424 m, 25 m before its slowest point (446 m, 101 km/h, −0.10 = outside half), then drifts to −0.43 at 470 m at steer 0.64–0.68. (2) New: at five bends the detection is a **false start** (706 / 718 / 732 m, 968 / 981 m, 1,454 / 1,466 m, 1,888 / 1,903 m, 2,919 / 2,928 m): the bearing passes 2°, the steer steps to 0.4 in two steps at 190–220 km/h under brake 0.7, the nose turns, the nose-relative bearing falls below 1°, the bend is dropped (target 0), the heading term swings the wheel the other way (−0.44 → +0.35 at 708–714 m; −0.40 → +0.53 at 969–978 m, with the brake released and 0.19–0.34 throttle for 5 steps), then the bend is detected again and the steer spikes to −0.73 (721 m, 170 km/h) / −0.78 (987 m, 194 km/h). (3) Above 105 km/h the v0.93 lap has 49 steps with \|steer\| over 0.62 (27 in 400–500 m, 10 in 700–800 m, 4 in 500–600 m, 4 at the flick entry, 2 each at 900–1,000 and 1,900–2,000 m). **Candidates tried (all-30 mean; base v0.93 73.908; all 0 of 30 off unless noted):** (a) *bend held on the track-relative bearing* (bearing minus the track direction, so the nose turning in cannot drop the bend; removes the false starts): detect and release on it 74.362; release only 74.338 (1 off, 1.358, `turn_grip_aero` +); either/both 74.429 (1 off, 1.804). **The false start is not a fault to remove: it is a delayed turn-in** (the counter-swing puts the car back outside; holding the bend turns in 12 m earlier, single lap +0.43 s, lost at 400–500, 700–900, flick and hairpin). (b) *later detection* (threshold 2° → 2.25 / 2.5 / 3): 73.992 / 74.065 / 74.253; raised only at speed (150–180 km/h) by 0.5 / 1 / 2: 74.018 / 74.105 / 74.581, from 170: 74.048; raised only off the brake by 0.5 / 1 / 2: 73.902 / 74.112 / 74.339, from 190 km/h by 1 / 3: 73.921 / 74.085; earlier (1.75°): 74.151 (max 0.920). On the single lap 2.5° gains 0.11 s by 900 m (kink 200–300 m −0.05) and gives it back at 1,400–1,600 m (+0.11), the flick (+0.08) and 2,900–3,000 m (+0.04). (c) *turn-in ramp* (the line term faded in over N steps from detection): N 5 / 10 / 20 / 40: 73.906 (paired −0.002 s, 16 of 30 faster) / 74.392 / 74.433 / 74.338; only above 150 km/h N 10 / 20: 74.381 / 74.386; above 170: 74.340 / 74.323. **A softer turn-in is 0.4–0.5 s slower: the sharp step is wanted.** (d) *steering rate scaled with 1/speed* above 80 / 120 / 150 / 180 / 210 km/h: 74.807 (1 off) / 74.058 / 73.933 / 73.906 / 73.946; only off the brake above 80 / 120: 74.038 / 73.944. (e) *3 → 2 downshift held while the car slides* (plan's item (b); no rpm downshift above \|speedY\| 4 / 6 / 9 km/h): 73.967 (max 0.908) / 73.936 / 73.905: flat. (f) *proportional lift* (own, new: in the lift band send the stored throttle faded with the excess instead of 0; width 0.3 / 0.6 / 1.0 of the band): 74.039 / 74.049 / 74.162, with `lift_pct` 2.5: 74.273: more drive while riding the plan is slower (as v0.89). (g) **steering cap at speed** (own, new; chosen): cap 0.45 / 0.55 / 0.58 / **0.62** / 0.66 / 0.70 from 105 km/h: 73.970 / 73.892 / 73.906 / **73.880** / 73.902 / 73.918; 0.62 from 90 km/h: 73.958 (max 0.837); 0.62 faded in over 95–125 km/h: 73.931. Item (c) of the plan (non-monotonic allowed speed, 113 → 117 → 102 km/h at 416–444 m on v0.93) was not trialled: the rise comes from the road ahead growing 25.9 → 28.0 m as the nose yaws, and a cap that removes it also cuts the 107–109 km/h the plan allows through 460–500 m (same road length); with the 3.5 % lift band it now costs one 0.11–0.14 brake touch of 8 steps. **Inherited conclusion challenged:** "the turn-in overshoot at detection costs time" (v0.89 / batch plan). Measured: every way of softening, delaying or holding it is slower; only the part of the step beyond ~0.6 steer is free to remove. |
| **Prediction** | Removing the steering beyond the front tyres' grip at speed costs nothing (cap 0.45 only +0.06 s) and gives a little more plan credit and throttle in the medium bends; a few hundredths at most. |
| **Lap time** | 1:13.78 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 285 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 56 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run `runs/run_20261003_155611.csv`; TORCS time 73.782 s → on screen 1:13:78 (v0.93 73.880: −0.098 s; best single lap so far, v0.92 was 73.820). Max \|trackPos\| 0.659 at ~2,477 m (flick; hairpin 0.56); slowest corner 56 km/h at ~3,274 m. Checkpoints vs v0.93: 700–800 m −0.058 s, 1,000–1,100 m −0.041, 1,100–1,200 m −0.023, 2,400–2,500 m +0.020; −0.150 s by 1,200 m. 786 m bend (740–800 m): 1.69 → 1.65 s, mean speed 122.7 → 125.7 km/h, mean allowed speed 131.6 → 135.2; turn-in spike at 720 m −0.73 → −0.62. 450 m bend (440–520 m): unchanged, 2.74 s at 107.8 km/h. **Suites:** 73.849 / 73.874 / 73.918, **all-30 73.880** (v0.93 73.908), 0 of 30 off, max 0.731 (`turn_grip_aero` +, 2,472 m) / 0.780 (`brake_margin` −2, 2,475 m) / 0.717 (`line_aim_off` −0.2, 2,473 m), all at the flick (v0.93: 0.725 / 0.802 / 0.703). **Paired per run vs v0.93: −0.028 s, 20 of 30 faster** (spread of the differences 0.146 s, standard error 0.027). **Shifted bases 0 of 40 off:** `brake_margin` 14: 73.809 / 73.789, all-20 73.799 (v0.93 73.898), max 0.840 / 0.857 (`brake_margin` a further −2, hairpin exit 3,278 m; v0.93 0.876); `line_offset` 0.5: 73.813 / 74.067, all-20 73.940 (v0.93 73.948), max 0.746 / 0.789. `tools/width.py` (entry −80 m / slowest point / exit +80 m, + = outside): 446 m +0.21 / +0.11 / +0.36 (was +0.21 / +0.11 / +0.37); 777 m +0.20 / **−0.18** / +0.29 (was −0.04; v0.92 −0.16); 1,038 m +0.38 / −0.45 / +0.20 (was −0.48); 1,525 m +0.09 / −0.39 / −0.13 (was −0.45); 1,929 m −0.21 (was −0.20); flick +0.65 (was +0.66); 2,989 m −0.46; hairpin −0.51 (was −0.48). `tools/patterns.py`: 45 steering reversals (was 38), 2 episodes, 0.7 s (was 0: 536–547 m, the set-up target flipping 0 / +0.85 / −0.85 out of the 450 m bend, and 3,283–3,292 m at the hairpin exit; 4 reversals each); 109 target flips (101); 104 plan jumps (97); 33 brake applications (35), 17 short touches (19), 16 re-applied within 10 steps (18); 46 shifts, 2 undone within 1 s (4); TC 15.1 s (14.6). |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — meets the bar (0 damage, 0 of 30 off, all-30 73.908 → 73.880, shifted bases 0 of 40 off with both shifted means lower and the on-track max 0.876 → 0.857), but **the gain is small and only weakly supported**: paired −0.028 s over the 30 (inside the ±0.03 s noise), −0.099 s and −0.008 s on the two shifted bases (about −0.04 s over the 70 runs); the neighbours 0.55–0.70 sit at 73.892–73.918. It does not fix the turn-in: the 448 m bend is unchanged. |
| **Learned** | (1) Above ~100 km/h steering beyond ~0.6 does nothing for the car: a cap of 0.45 costs only 0.06 s over 30 laps, 0.55–0.70 nothing. The front tyres are saturated in the 450 m bend (0.64–0.68 held for 80 m while the car drifts out) and in the turn-in spikes. (2) The cap also acts through two readers of the steer: the sharpness plan's credit fades from 0.61 (`turn_steer_fade`) and the throttle is limited from `lock_steer` 0.6, so a capped wheel keeps more plan credit (786 m bend: allowed +3.6 km/h, speed +3.0 km/h). (3) The turn-in at detection is a robust local optimum of today's design: holding the bend (no false start), detecting later or earlier, ramping the line term in, and slowing the steering rate at speed all lose 0.1–0.5 s; the false start at 190–220 km/h works as a delayed turn-in. What is left for the 448 m bend is the path itself (inside reached 25 m before the slowest point), not the shape of the step. (4) Lap-to-lap chaos is large against these effects: single laps of flat variants ran 73.72–74.02 with all-30 means within 0.03 s of each other. (5) The two new short steering episodes (536–547 m, 3,283–3,292 m) are 4 reversals each and come from the set-up target flipping at a bend exit, not from the cap. (6) TC cut time (14.6 s on v0.93, 15.1 now) is mostly the lift band: the stored throttle stays at 1.0 through each lift, so each resumption spins the rear and is cut (450 m bend: sent throttle 0 / 0.5–0.7 alternating every 2–3 steps at 471–530 m); sending part throttle in the band instead is slower (candidate f). |

## v0.95 — Higher planned deceleration at speed (`brake_max_hi` 34 up to 235 km/h)

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.95 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.95`). One mechanism in the braking plan: **the cap on the planned deceleration is 34 m/s² instead of 30 wherever that only lifts the allowed speed up to 235 km/h**. New knobs `brake_max_hi` **34** (line 541) and `brake_hi_v` **235** km/h (line 542). The old `brake_speed(d)` body became `brake_dist_speed(d, a_max)` (lines 820–826, same arithmetic with the cap as an argument) and `brake_speed(d)` is now `min(brake_dist_speed(d, brake_max_hi), max(brake_dist_speed(d, brake_max), brake_hi_v/3.6))` (lines 827–828; comment 808–819), used as before for the road ahead and for every beam of the sharpness plan. `brake_max` stays 30. Line mapping from v0.94: lines 1–540 unchanged; old 541–805 move +2; old 806–812 (the function) are now 820–828; old ≥ 813 move +16. No other value changed. |
| **Why** | Own analysis first, of the v0.94 run and of the simulator source. **(1) The 450 m bend is flat against its speed plan** (challenges the inherited "what is left at 448 m is the path / the plan is not monotonic"): a position-gated diagnostic that adds an offset to the allowed speed in a window (single laps, time at 600 m against the base): 432–505 m −8 / −5 / −3 / +3 / +6 km/h → +0.01 / +0.02 / +0.05 / +0.01 / +0.11 s; 405–450 m −5 / −8 / +5 → +0.03 / +0.05 / +0.06; 440–490 m −5 → +0.04; 490–530 m +5 → +0.02; 405–470 m −5 → +0.06. With −8 km/h the car holds −0.08 instead of −0.29 at 505 m and gains nothing: neither a slower entry (later apex) nor more speed pays there, so no version was spent on that path. **(2) The road ahead measured along the direction of travel** (own, new: `ahead` from the beam at `ahead_slip` × the slip angle instead of the nose; it removes the plan's rise at 416–424 m while the car slides at 8°): all-30 73.864 / 73.877 / 73.879 at 0.3 / 0.6 / 1.0 and 73.854 with `corner_speed` 80 (max 0.917) against 73.880: flat. **(3) Stored throttle bled while lifting with the wheel turned** (the brief's item 3): 0.03 / 0.08 / 0.2 per step → 73.914 / 74.008 / 74.039: slower, monotonic; with v0.89 (plan-riding throttle) and v0.94 (part throttle in the band) that is three ways of smoothing the resumption that lose: the bursts of rear slip help turn a front-limited car. **(4) Wheelspin and the tyre model** (own, new; simuv2 `wheel.cpp` line 225 and `categories.cpp`): the tyre force is the magic formula times (1 + 0.3·slip) for a driver without a `skill level` entry (`scr_server` defaults to semi-pro), slip capped at 1.5, so the force keeps rising past the curve's peak (rear tyre: 1.07 at slip 0.2, 1.10 at 0.5, 1.14 at 1.0, 1.21 at 1.5 of the peak): that is why every traction-control cut on a straight has lost since v0.71. Single laps with `launch_slip` 10 / 5 / 2 / 0: −0.09 / +0.10 / +0.37 / +0.39 s; all-30 with `launch_slip` 8 / 10 / 13 / 17 / 40: 73.931 / 73.902 / 73.913 / 73.919 / 73.876: flat, nothing to gain there. **(5) The same question for braking led to the chosen change.** Decelerations measured on single laps while braking nearly straight (\|steer\| < 0.1, 4-step differences, flick approach excluded): v0.94 31.2 m/s² at 160–240 km/h and 34.6 at 240–280 with a mean pedal of 0.65; with `brake_gain` 0.1 37.7 at 200–240 (pedal 0.81) and 42.0 at 240–280 (0.94); with `brake_gain` 0.2 and no ABS 39–43 from 160 to 280 at pedal 1.0. The "27–30 m/s² at 200–240 km/h" behind `brake_max` 30 was therefore measured at the pedal the plan itself asked for (pedal = `brake_gain` × excess over the plan), not at the car's limit, and the cap binds above ~187 km/h. **Trials (all-30, base 73.880):** `brake_max` 34 / 38 alone 73.811 / 73.797 but 1 of 30 off each (`turn_grip_aero` +, flick); with `brake_gain` 0.075: `brake_max` 30 / 34 / 38 / 42 → 74.178 / 74.113 / 74.030 / 74.165 (3 off); with `brake_gain` 0.1: 34 / 38 / 42 / 46 → 74.344 (1 off) / 74.341 (1 off) / 74.400 (2 off) / 74.467 (5 off): a higher pedal gain is slower, the car running 10–15 km/h above the plan in the braking zones is itself lap time. **Why the flick fails:** on its approach (2,367–2,440 m) the pedal is at 1.0 and ABS-cut to 0.5 on every step for 70 m (crest, kink at \|steer\| 0.62), 20–50 km/h over the plan, so the arc-entry speed is set by the plan's two early dips (2,288–2,298 m and 2,333 m, 250 km/h with 54 m in view); with the cap at 34 the 2,333 m dip goes and the car reaches 2,365 m at 256 instead of 246 km/h. Gates tried: extra cap faded out with the crest load (`brake_add_load` 0.8–0.95, +4 / +8 / +12): 73.816–73.940, the same run off every time (the 2,333 m dip is not on a crest reading); ABS on the second-slowest wheel: 73.937 alone, 73.885 / 73.905 with `brake_max` 34 / 38 and still 1 off (on the third-slowest: 77.2, 3 off). **The gate that holds: the higher cap counts only up to a speed** (`brake_hi_v`): 34 / 235 → 73.840, 0 off; 34 / 245 → 73.867, 2 off (`turn_grip_aero` +, `brake_aero` +); 38 / 235 → 73.830, max 0.825; 38 / 225 → 73.844, max 0.891. Chosen 34 / 235 (lowest max, both neighbours on speed measured). |
| **Prediction** | Braking 1–3 m later in every zone that starts below ~260 km/h, the flick approach unchanged above 235 km/h; a few hundredths over the lap. |
| **Lap time** | 1:13.72 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 285 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 54 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run `runs/run_20261003_163454.csv`; TORCS time 73.728 s → on screen 1:13:72 (v0.94 73.782: −0.054 s; best single lap so far). Max \|trackPos\| 0.673 at ~2,476 m (flick; hairpin 0.55); slowest corner 54 km/h at ~3,273 m. Largest section changes vs v0.94: 200–300 m −0.044 s, 700–800 m +0.044, 3,300–3,400 m −0.044, 1,500–1,600 m −0.024, 900–1,000 m +0.022, 3,500–3,600 m −0.022. Brake onsets move 0–2 m later (e.g. 689 → 690 m at 228 → 231 km/h, 959 → 961 m, 1,402 → 1,404 m); mean deceleration at 200–240 km/h 31.2 → 33.4 m/s² (pedal 0.64 → 0.71). **Suites (on the committed driver):** 73.778 / 73.862 / 73.881, **all-30 73.840** (v0.94 73.880), 0 of 30 off, max 0.770 (`turn_grip_aero` +, flick left arc, 2,462 m) / 0.755 (`brake_margin` −2, hairpin exit, 3,277 m) / 0.712 (`downshift_rpm` +500, flick, 2,477 m) (v0.94: 0.731 / 0.780 / 0.717). **Paired per run vs v0.94: −0.040 s (standard error 0.024), 19 of 30 faster**; per suite −0.070 / −0.012 / −0.037; one run (`line_offset` +0.1) is −0.44 s, without it the mean is −0.026 s. **Shifted bases: 0 of 40 off**; `brake_margin` 14: 73.809 / 73.812 (all-20 73.811; v0.94 73.799), max 0.863 (`turn_grip_aero` +, 2,462 m) / 0.895 (`brake_margin` further −2, hairpin exit 3,278 m) (v0.94 on-track max 0.857); `line_offset` 0.5: 73.787 / 73.764 (73.775; v0.94 73.940), max 0.868 (`turn_grip` +0.5, 2,462 m) / 0.774 (v0.94 0.789). **Patterns** (`tools/patterns.py`, v0.94 → v0.95): steering reversals 45 → 42, oscillation episodes 2 (0.7 s) → 0, target flips 109 → 103, plan jumps > 20 km/h 104 → 98, brake applications 33 → 35 of which short touches 17 → 23 (worse) and re-applied within 10 steps 16 → 16, shifts 46 → 47 (2 → 4 undone within 1 s), TC cut time 15.1 → 14.9 s. **Width** (`tools/width.py`, entry / slowest point / exit): 448 m +0.21 / +0.13 / +0.35 (was +0.21 / +0.11 / +0.36), 787 m +0.19 / −0.00 / +0.27 (was 777 m +0.20 / −0.18 / +0.29), 1,040 m +0.37 / −0.46 / +0.19, 1,527 m +0.10 / −0.46 / −0.10 (was −0.39), 1,928 m +0.30 / −0.16 / +0.08, flick +0.66, 2,989 m −0.47, hairpin −0.49 at 54 km/h: the line is unchanged apart from the 787 m bend, which is back on the centre at its slowest point (as on v0.93). ~1,330 races. |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — meets the bar (0 damage, 0 of 30 off, all-30 73.880 → 73.840, paired −0.040 s with 19 of 30 faster, shifted bases 0 of 40 off), but **the gain is small and only weakly supported**: 1.7 standard errors, −0.026 s without one run; over the 70 runs about −0.06 s, of which most is the `line_offset` 0.5 base (−0.165 s) while the `brake_margin` 14 base is +0.012 s. **It spends margin on the shifted bases** (on-track max 0.857 → 0.895 at the hairpin exit, 0.789 → 0.868 at the flick) and the gate has a cliff 10 km/h away (`brake_hi_v` 245: 2 of 30 off). Short brake touches rose 17 → 23: the plan now has an edge at 235 km/h (the allowed speed sits on `brake_hi_v` for a stretch of each big braking zone). |
| **Learned** | (1) `brake_max` 30 was not the car's limit: at full pedal the car slows at 39–43 m/s² from 160 to 280 km/h; the old figure was measured at pedal ~0.65, which is all a plan capped at 30 asks for with `brake_gain` 0.05. (2) Most of that cannot be used today: a higher pedal gain is slower at every cap (74.03–74.47), and a higher cap alone leaves the track at the flick, whose approach is pedal-saturated and ABS-cut for 70 m and is protected only by two early dips of the plan (2,288–2,298 m, 2,333 m). The flick approach is the plan's weakest stretch: its arc-entry speed is decided 100 m earlier by a dip, not by the braking itself. (3) The tyre force in this simulator rises with slip beyond the curve's peak (× (1 + 0.3·slip), slip up to 1.5, for the default semi-pro skill level): wheelspin on a straight costs nothing, which explains every lost traction-control trial, and locked wheels would brake harder than ABS (no ABS and pedal 1.0: 39–43 m/s², but the fronts then do not steer: 77–80 s laps, wide at 1,510 m). (4) The 450 m bend is flat against its speed plan in every window (±3 to ±8 km/h: +0.01 to +0.11 s by 600 m): the remaining time there is not in the entry speed or the apex position. (5) The road ahead along the direction of travel, bleeding the stored throttle in lifts and `launch_slip` 8–40 are flat or slower (numbers in Why). (6) ABS on the second-slowest wheel is 0.06 s slower and does not protect the flick. |

## v0.96 — Softer traction-control cut (`tc_gain` 0.5 → 0.3)

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.96 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.96`). One mechanism, one value: the traction-control gain `tc_gain` **0.5 → 0.3** (line 599): the sent throttle is cut by 0.3 instead of 0.5 per m/s of rear over-speed beyond the limit (`c.tc_cut`, line 1035, unchanged code). No line moved (line mapping from v0.95: identical); no other value changed. |
| **Why** | Own analysis first, of the v0.95 run with wheel speeds logged (a logging variant, byte-identical lap). **(1) The flick approach, braking capacity** (the batch's item 1; own measurement): at 2,393–2,424 m (wheel straight, \|steer\| < 0.15) both front wheels sit at 0.71–0.85 of the car speed, i.e. at the tyre's peak slip, with the pedal already cut to 0.5 on most steps, while the rears turn at 0.91–0.97 (slip 0.03–0.09): the fronts are grip-limited there (27–31 m/s² at 200 → 135 km/h, crest load 0.7–0.8), the rears are under-braked by the brake balance; the cut is not a false trigger. **ABS released while the wheel is straight** (own, new: no cut below \|steer\| `abs_steer` − `abs_fade`): all-30 73.959 / 73.971 / 74.031 / 74.217 at `abs_steer` 0.10 / 0.15 / 0.25 / 0.40 against 73.840, 0 of 30 off, max 0.759–0.734: slower the more it is released (the car following the plan more closely in the braking zones is itself lap time, as with a higher `brake_gain` in v0.95); with the higher deceleration cap ungated on top (`brake_hi_v` 400, `brake_max_hi` 34 / 38 / 42): 73.859 / 73.908 with 1 of 30 off (`turn_grip_aero` +) / 74.050: no gain, the flick still fails at 38. **(2) The flick approach, timing of the plan** (own, new; a position-gated diagnostic, not a driver mechanism): the speed trace there is a sawtooth (brake 275 → 256 km/h at 2,288–2,298 m, coast at 250 to 2,333 m, brake touch, coast at 244, brake to 232 at 2,352 m, coast to 2,374 m), which by distance alone looks like ~0.1 s against braking once and late. Forcing the plan open (suite 1, base 73.778): 2,300–2,345 m → 73.839, max 0.919 (slower and wider); 2,280–2,320 m → 6 of 10 off at the flick; 2,280–2,335 m → 10 of 10 off; 2,280–2,345 m and longer → **damage at 2,351 m on every run with the car still on the road** (the compression before the crest, vertical acceleration +12 m/s², taken at ~280 km/h). So the early dips are not false: the approach has a real speed limit near 245–250 km/h at 2,350 m, and the inherited reading "an accident protecting the flick" is replaced by "the dips are in the right place for a reason the plan does not model". No version spent there. **(3) The tightened arc** (the batch's item 2, banked from v0.93; re-built: the arc to a beam takes the tightest inner-beam radius and the shorter chord instead of being discarded): all-30 73.791, max 0.806 on v0.95 (−0.049 s, inside the noise); with `tc_gain` 0.3: 73.733 against 73.688 (+0.045): it does not stack. **(4) Traction control, chosen.** The per-run table of the v0.95 suites showed the fastest perturbed runs were the ones that loosen traction control (`tc_slip_straight` +1: 73.532, `tc_slip` +0.2: 73.620, base lap 73.728), which fits v0.95's reading of the tyre model (force × (1 + 0.3·slip): it keeps rising past the curve's peak) and the rise of the cut time since the wide lift band of v0.93 (11.9 → 14.9 s: every resumption at stored throttle 1.0 spins the rears). **Trials (all-30, base 73.840, all 0 of 30 off unless said):** `tc_slip_straight` 6 / 7 / 8 / 11 / 15 → 73.764 / 73.776 / 73.741 / 73.794 / 73.774 (max 0.786 / 0.874 / 0.789 / 0.868 / 0.917); `tc_slip` 4.8 / 5.2 / 5.6 / 6.2 → 73.774 / 73.740 / 73.742 / 73.744 (max 0.866 / 0.896 / 0.856 / 0.866); `tc_slip` 5.2 or 5.6 with `tc_slip_straight` 8 → 73.742 / 73.693 (max 0.904 / 0.892); `tc_vref` 95 → 73.753 (max 0.912); **`tc_gain` 0.4 / 0.3 / 0.2 / 0.1 / 0 → 73.743 / 73.688 / 73.704 / 73.983 / 29 of 30 off (max 0.773 / 0.789 / 0.861 / 0.981 / –)**; `tc_gain` 0.3 with `tc_hold` 0.7 / 0.9 → 73.711 / 73.837; with `tc_slip_straight` 8 → 73.673 (max 0.783); with `tc_slip` 5.6 → 73.726 (max 0.846). Every loosening is faster than the base; the gain is the one that costs no margin (a higher limit moves the suite max to 0.86–0.92, the softer cut leaves it at 0.79). Chosen `tc_gain` 0.3: the lowest mean among the single-knob changes, both neighbours measured (0.4 and 0.2 pass), and the cliff (0.1: 0.981) two steps away. The older results "`tc_gain` 0.4 flat on v0.78", "`tc_gain` 0 / 0.25 slower or off (batch 6)" were on cars without the clutch slip in every gear, the slip-ratio limit (v0.83) and the wide lift band (v0.93). |
| **Prediction** | Less drive lost in each traction-control cut (out of the bends that ride the plan and at the corner exits), a few hundredths per exit; slides at the flick and the hairpin exit to be checked, since the cut also damps them. |

| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 285 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 55 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run `runs/run_20261003_165350.csv`; TORCS time 73.590 s → on screen 1:13:59 (v0.95 73.728: −0.138 s; best single lap so far). Max \|trackPos\| 0.679 at ~2,476 m (flick; hairpin 0.56); slowest corner 55 km/h at ~3,273 m. Section changes vs v0.95 are spread over the lap: 200–300 m −0.044 s, 100–200 m −0.022, 400–500 m −0.020, 900–1,000 m −0.022, 2,400–2,600 m −0.040, 3,200–3,300 m −0.022, against 1,200–1,300 m +0.022 and 3,300–3,400 m +0.044 (hairpin exit: 161.1 instead of 162.7 km/h at 3,340 m). Steps with a traction-control cut 655 → 656, mean cut 0.22 → 0.20 of throttle. **Hairpin trace** (first gear at full lock, deliberate rear slide): 1st at 3,257–3,270 m, slide 22.6 km/h at 3,272 m (v0.95 23.9), \|trackPos\| 0.56 (0.55), sent throttle 0.32–0.62 out of the arc at stored 1.0, straight from 3,293 m: the same shape. **Suites (on the committed driver):** 73.687 / 73.719 / 73.658, **all-30 73.688** (v0.95 73.840), 0 of 30 off, max 0.761 (`turn_grip_aero` +, flick, 2,473 m) / 0.789 (`brake_margin` −2, flick, 2,475 m) / 0.749 (`line_aim_off` −0.2, flick, 2,476 m) (v0.95: 0.770 / 0.755 / 0.712). **Paired per run vs v0.95: −0.153 s (standard error 0.031), 24 of 30 faster**; per suite −0.092 / −0.143 / −0.223; the largest single difference is `line_aim_off` +0.2 (−0.73 s: 74.922 on v0.95, a 1.2 s outlier there), without it the mean is −0.133 s; slower runs: `tc_slip_straight` +1 (+0.10) and `line_offset` −0.1 (+0.09) the largest. **Shifted bases: 0 of 40 off**; `brake_margin` 14: 73.843 / 73.730 (all-20 73.787; v0.95 73.811), max 0.863 (`turn_grip_aero` +, flick left arc, 2,462 m) / 0.893 (`brake_margin` further −2, hairpin exit, 3,278 m) (v0.95 on-track max 0.895); `line_offset` 0.5: 73.754 / 73.877 (73.815; v0.95 73.775), max 0.876 (`turn_grip_aero` +, 2,462 m) / 0.756 (v0.95 0.868 / 0.774). **Patterns** (`tools/patterns.py`, v0.95 → v0.96): steering reversals 42 → 41, oscillation episodes 0 → 1 (0.3 s at 536–546 m, the set-up target flipping at the 450 m exit, as on v0.94: worse), target flips 103 → 102, plan jumps > 20 km/h 98 → 99, brake applications 35 → 34 of which short touches 23 → 21 and re-applied within 10 steps 16 → 14, shifts 47 → 47 (4 → 3 undone within 1 s), TC cut time 14.9 → 14.6 s. **Width** (`tools/width.py`, entry / slowest point / exit): 446 m +0.21 / +0.11 / +0.36 (was +0.21 / +0.13 / +0.35), 770 m +0.21 / −0.15 / +0.30 (was 787 m +0.19 / −0.00 / +0.27), 1,042 m +0.37 / −0.46 / +0.19, 1,528 m +0.09 / −0.41 / −0.02 (was −0.46 / −0.10), 1,927 m +0.25 / −0.14 / +0.10, flick +0.67, 2,988 m −0.46, hairpin −0.49 at 55 km/h: the line is unchanged apart from the 770 m bend, back inside at its slowest point (as on v0.94). ~1,220 races. |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — meets the bar: 0 damage, 0 of 30 off, all-30 73.840 → 73.688, paired −0.153 s at 5 standard errors with 24 of 30 faster (−0.133 s without the one outlier), shifted bases 0 of 40 off with the on-track max unchanged (0.895 → 0.893); best single lap (1:13.59). **The gain is smaller on the shifted bases** (`brake_margin` 14: −0.024 s, `line_offset` 0.5: +0.040 s; over all 70 runs about −0.06 s), so the all-30 figure is probably flattered by a few hundredths. Concerns: one short steering oscillation episode is back (536–546 m, 0.3 s); the suite max rose 0.770 → 0.789 (still the flick); `tc_gain` has a cliff at 0.1 (max 0.981) and the suites only perturb it upward (+0.1). |
| **Learned** | (1) Traction control was tuned too tight for the present car: every loosening tried (higher limit on straights or in turns, lower reference speed, softer cut) is 0.05–0.15 s faster over 30 perturbed laps; the softer cut is the one that keeps the margin, because the limit still sets where the cut starts and the hold (`tc_hold` 0.8) still damps the slide; no cut at all leaves the track on 29 of 30. (2) The per-run suite table is a free sensitivity scan: the perturbations that beat the base lap pointed straight at the gain. (3) The flick approach's early dips are not an accident: with the plan forced open to 2,345 m the car takes damage at 2,351 m (compression before the crest at ~280 km/h) and to 2,320–2,335 m it leaves the track at the flick; opening only 2,300–2,345 m is slower. The approach is limited near 245–250 km/h at 2,350 m, so a higher deceleration cap above 235 km/h has nothing to win there. (4) On the flick approach the ABS cut is real front lock (fronts at peak slip with the pedal at 0.5, rears at slip 0.03–0.09): the car is front-grip-limited at 27–31 m/s² over the crest; releasing the ABS with a straight wheel brakes harder and is slower (73.96–74.22). (5) The tightened arc is −0.05 s alone on v0.95 (inside the noise) and does not stack with the softer cut. (6) `line_aim_off` +0.2 was a 1.2 s outlier on v0.95 (74.922, time lost at 200, 1,000–1,200, 1,500 and 2,900–3,000 m: the bend hold ending early) and is 74.194 here: that knob has a cliff just above 1.0. |

each of them as a new base is slower over 30 runs (below). **All trials: all-30 mean, paired per-run difference to v0.96 (standard error), runs faster of 30, suite max; base 73.688, max 0.789; 0 of 30 off unless said.** **(1) Sharpness-plan grip:** `turn_grip` 8.5 → 73.763, +0.075 (0.032), 12, max 0.918; `tc_slip_slide` 14 / 30 → 73.710 / 73.714, +0.022 / +0.026, 11 / 13, max 0.773 / 0.783; `tc_slip_steer` 0.5 / 1.0 → 73.725 / 73.702, +0.037 / +0.014, 10 / 15, max 0.856 / 0.882; `tc_vref` 100 / 120 → 73.695 / 73.818, +0.007 / +0.130, 17 / 3, max 0.785 / 0.767; `apex_steer` 0.45 / 0.55 → 73.739 / 73.730, +0.051 / +0.042, 11 / 11, max 0.799 / 0.900; `line_isteer` 0.5 → 73.877, +0.190 (0.048), 4, max 0.859; the suites' own `turn_steer_fade` ±0.05 and `turn_steer_max` −0.05 runs are 73.590 / 73.586 / 73.580 against 73.590 (the fade window is dead above 105 km/h, as v0.94 read it, and re-opening it is worth nothing). **(4) Line knobs** (item 3): `line_gain` 0.6 → 73.881, +0.193 (0.049), 5; `lookahead_gain` 2.3 → 73.822, +0.134 (0.033), 7. **(5) Brake pedal:** `brake_gain` 0.045 / 0.04 → 73.755 / 73.714, +0.068 / +0.026, 8 / 13, max 0.861 / 0.936 (hairpin exit). **(6) Own, new: the exits of the medium bends** (step trace of the 450 m bend's exit, 500–552 m): at 500–530 m the car is on the outside half (trackPos −0.37, left-hand bend), sliding at 17–19 km/h with the nose 8° into the bend at 115–123 km/h and the sent throttle cut to 0.6–0.75, held there by the released line target (+0.18 steer, pulling the car back toward the centre) and the inside integral at its +0.4 cap; when the bend ends at 530 m (bearing under `line_aim_off`) both go and the steering steps −0.03 → −0.33 in two steps, the car straightens within 8 steps and the cut disappears; the set-up for the next bend then flips its side at 536–543 m (the one oscillation episode). Three variants (code changes, byte-identical to v0.96 when off): **no inward line pull once the exit is released** (`exit_free` 0.5 / 1.0: the line term is scaled down by the released share while it pulls toward the inside) → 73.706 / 73.698, +0.019 / +0.011, 14 / 14, max 0.859 / 0.891; **inside integral let go with the release** (`exit_i` 0.5 / 1.0) → 73.822 / 73.882, +0.135 / +0.194, 5 / 2; both together → 73.906, +0.218, 2, max 0.968; **line term faded out after the bend's end instead of dropped** (kept ×0.7 / ×0.9 per step) → 73.799 / 74.027, +0.111 / +0.339, 9 / 0 of 30. So the slide held by the integral on the exit and the steering step at the bend's end are both worth lap time; the smoother the handover, the slower (the same sign as the turn-in step, v0.94, and the throttle bursts, v0.93–v0.95). **(7) Own, new: throttle ramp rate** (the stored throttle's +0.05 per step, last measured in batch 7; with the softer cut a faster ramp could have paid): 0.04 / 0.065 → 73.730 / 73.727, +0.043 / +0.039, 12 / 13, max 0.922 / 0.876. **(8) The 1,042 m bend's two-stage braking** (955–996 m: brake 237 → 211 km/h, release, then pedal 1.0 at steer 0.62 from 207 km/h, 30 km/h over the plan) is the false-start bend detection of v0.94 finding (a) (bend detected at 969 m, dropped at 975 m as the nose yaws in, steering to +0.51 the wrong way for 4 steps, re-detected at 982 m); v0.94 measured every way of holding it as slower, so no trial was spent there. **Result: 37 neighbours of v0.96, one below the base and inside the noise.** `tc_slip_straight` 8 was taken as the candidate and measured on the shifted bases as well. |

The per-run suite table pointed at no gain this time: the fastest perturbed single runs (`turn_grip` +0.5, `tc_slip` +0.2) are slower as bases. Since v0.96's 73.688 is the best of many neighbours, it is probably flattered by a few hundredths; knob re-scans are finished until a mechanism changes the car. (2) **The rough handovers are functional, on exits as on entries**: on the exit of the medium bends the inside integral holds the car in a 17–19 km/h slide, nose 8° in, with the throttle cut, and the bend's end drops 0.3 of steering in two steps; releasing the inward pull is flat (+0.01 to +0.02), releasing the integral or fading the line term out is 0.11–0.34 s slower. With the turn-in step (v0.94) and the throttle bursts (v0.93–v0.95) that is the fourth place where smoothing loses: on this car the yaw the controller forces is what turns it, and the remaining time is not in smoother control. (3) `line_aim_off` 1.0 is a sharp optimum: 0.9 / 0.8 lose 0.11 / 0.32 s, +0.2 loses 0.5 s on its suite run; `setup_pull` 0.15 likewise (0.10: +0.21 s and a run off at the flick; 0.18 / 0.22: +0.04 / +0.06 with the suite max at 0.89 / 0.94). (4) The traction-control knobs other than the gain are flat at gain 0.3 (±0.04 s); a higher limit on straights moves the margin place on the `brake_margin` 14 base from the hairpin exit (0.893) to the flick's left arc (0.928). (5) The fade window of the sharpness plan (`turn_steer_max` / `turn_steer_fade`) is dead above 105 km/h since the steering cap and that costs nothing (±0.05: ±0.01 s on the single runs). |

## v0.98 — Held bend in the medium bends (new `hold_n` 15 / `hold_v0` 125 / `hold_v1` 175 / `hold_road` 50 / `hold_aim` 0.5)

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.98 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.98`). One mechanism: **an established bend is not dropped mid-bend.** A bend that has been held for at least `hold_n` **15** steps is kept, although the look-ahead bearing has fallen below `line_aim_off` (1°), while the speed is between `hold_v0` **125** and `hold_v1` **175** km/h, less than `hold_road` **50** m of road is visible along the track direction and the bearing still points more than `hold_aim` **0.5°** toward the bend's side (new knobs, lines 553–557; test at lines 664–673, with the `road` line moved up to 663; step counter `c.bend_n` at line 770). The false starts at turn-in (bend held 3–5 steps) are untouched, as are the flick (kink at 180–230 km/h, arcs below 105) and the hairpin. Line mapping from v0.96: old 553–656 +5, old 663 (`road`) → 663, old 657–662 → 662 / 670–675, old 664–757 +12, old ≥ 758 +13. No existing value changed. **Rejected: the driver was restored to v0.96 afterwards** (`git checkout v0.96 -- snakeoil3_v1.py`). |
| **Why** | The batch plan gave v0.98 to **false-start bend detection as a plan problem**. Own analysis first, of the v0.96 run and then of the 30 suite runs kept as telemetry. **(1) The plan's premise does not hold in the step traces.** Of the false starts above 150 km/h on the v0.96 lap (dropped / re-detected at 694 / 705, 710 / 719, 976 / 982, 1,461 / 1,468, 2,921 / 2,928 m, plus a drop and re-detection 28 m apart at 2,630 / 2,658 m, not traced) the allowed speed rises while the bend is dropped at **one** only (976 m: 203.7 → 212.3 km/h as the nose yaws into the bend and the nose beams grow 54 → 60 m; the car coasts 5 steps, 210.9 → 207.4 km/h). At the other four the plan does not rise at all (rise 0.0–0.3 km/h): the car is 10–25 km/h over a falling plan throughout, braking at pedal 0.4–0.9 with the ABS cutting every other step. The collapse after re-detection (worst plan − speed −17 to −39 km/h) is the nose swinging back out under the wrong-way steer, which shortens every beam. **Measured as plan mechanisms** (variant with the switch off reproduces all-30 73.688): a hold of the plan at its value at the drop for 8 / 16 steps, keyed to the dropped-bend event only (bend held ≤ 10 steps, above 150 km/h): **73.693 / 73.692** (paired +0.005 / +0.004 s, SE 0.011, 13 of 30 faster, max 0.789): nothing; a limit on how fast the plan may fall for 6 steps after the re-detection (2.5 / 1.5 km/h per step): **73.969 / 74.281** (paired +0.281 / +0.594 s; the car then arrives too fast and washes out). The collapse is a real limit, not an accident. **(2) Plan item 2, rear braking**: downshift at `brake_ds_rpm` whenever the ABS is cutting (any speed / above 150 km/h): **73.761 / 73.764** (+0.073 / +0.076 s, 6 of 30 faster); `brake_ds_rpm` 18,200 / 16,500: 73.714 / 73.776 (max 0.810 / 0.916); `brake_ds_over` 15 / 28: 73.771 / 73.847 (max 0.900 / 0.989): the v0.86 values are still the optimum on this car. **(3) Plan item 3, straight-line traction-control limit gated by speed**: +3 m/s only above 130–160 km/h: **73.738** (+0.050 s, SE 0.020, max 0.877 at 2,462 m); only below 130–160 km/h: **73.699** (22 of 30 faster, typically −0.05 to −0.15 s, but +0.70 s on `turn_grip` +0.5 and +0.59 s on `line_aim_off` +0.2; max 0.901 at 2,462 m); only below 90–120 km/h: 73.708; +1.5 below 130–160: 73.750 (max 0.903). No speed gate separates the gain from the flick: on the `turn_grip` +0.5 run the car reaches 2,380 m 5 km/h faster (231.8 against 226.5 km/h), holds steer −0.47 to −0.62 under ABS braking to 2,405 m (−0.07 to −0.45 on the base run) and enters the left arc at 104 instead of 100 km/h (0.901 at 2,462 m, lap +0.70 s). **(4) Own, new: where the suite runs differ.** Section times of the 30 kept suite runs: the sections whose time goes with the lap time are 200–300 m (start-straight kink: 1.428 s on most runs, up to 1.598), 1,500–1,600 m (2.307–2.447 s, correlation with the lap 0.62) and 2,900–3,100 m. At 1,500–1,600 m six runs lose 0.03–0.13 s, and on the worst (`brake_gain` +0.01, `line_aim_off` +0.2) the cause is a **mid-bend drop**: in this steady left bend the bearing sits at −1.14 to −1.16° (threshold 1°); on those runs the car is 0.05 further inside, the bearing touches −1.00 at 1,508 m at 144 km/h, the bend is dropped, the line term and the inside integral go, the steering steps +0.20 → 0.00 → −0.15, the nose swings out (angle −0.093 → +0.017), the plan falls 144 → 128 km/h, the car brakes (0.43–0.57), shifts down to 2nd at 1,517 m and runs 1,518–1,560 m about 10 km/h slower (0.11 s). Holding an established bend is this version. Ungated (any speed) it is slower: 73.796–73.855 with the suite max up to 0.933 at the flick's left arc (it holds the bend through the flick's kink); with more road allowed (80 m) 74.105; above 110 km/h 73.708–73.722 (max 0.809–0.933); above 125 km/h 73.679 (max 0.868); **between 125 and 175 km/h 73.676 with every margin unchanged** (`hold_aim` 0 / 0.3 / 0.5, `hold_road` 40 / 50 and `hold_n` 8 / 15 give identical laps); between 110 and 175 km/h 73.725. **Challenged:** the inherited "while the bend is dropped the plan rises" (v0.97 finding c, generalised to five bends in the batch plan): true at 976 m only, by 2 km/h above the value at the drop, and holding it there is worth nothing. |
| **Prediction** | About −0.02 s on all-30 from the six runs that lose 0.03–0.13 s at 1,500–1,600 m; nothing on the unperturbed lap (it has no mid-bend drop); no change at the flick or the hairpin. |
| **Lap time** | 1:13.59 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 285 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 55 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run `runs/run_20261003_174828.csv`; TORCS time 73.590 s → on screen 1:13:59. **Identical to the v0.96 run in all 3,605 rows** (the hold never acts on the unperturbed lap). Max \|trackPos\| 0.679 at ~2,476 m (flick; hairpin 0.56); slowest corner 55 km/h at ~3,273 m. **Suites on the committed driver** (v0.96 in brackets): 73.663 / 73.704 / 73.661 (73.687 / 73.719 / 73.658), **all-30 73.676** (73.688), max 0.761 / 0.789 / 0.749 (unchanged; 0.789 = `brake_margin` −2, flick 2,475 m), **0 of 30 off**. Paired per run against v0.96: 30 suite runs **−0.012 s** (SE 0.010; 3 faster, 3 slower, 24 identical: `line_offset` +0.1 −0.218, `brake_gain` +0.01 −0.182, `turn_grip_aero` − −0.022, `line_gain` +0.1 +0.034, `line_aim_off` +0.2 +0.028, `brake_margin` +2 +0.002). **Shifted bases 0 of 40 off**: bm14 73.808 (73.787), max 0.893 (`brake_margin` further −2, hairpin exit 3,278 m, unchanged); lo05 73.807 (73.815), max 0.876 (`turn_grip_aero` +, flick left arc 2,462 m, unchanged); paired over the 40: **+0.007 s** (SE 0.017; 4 faster, 3 slower, 33 identical; bm14 `lookahead_gain` −0.4 +0.596, `line_gain` +0.1 +0.128, `brake_margin` +2 −0.172, `lock_throttle` +0.05 −0.124; lo05 `brake_margin` +2 −0.228, `line_offset` +0.1 +0.086, `line_gain` +0.1 −0.024). **All 70: −0.001 s** (SE 0.011; 7 faster, 6 slower, 57 identical). Mid-bend drops over the 30 suite runs (bend held ≥ 15 steps, dropped with < 50 m of road above 110 km/h and re-detected within 1 s): 34 → 27, of them followed by a brake over 0.3: 5 → 2, by a downshift: 4 → 1 (the 1,504–1,508 m drops are gone; left: 2,736 m on `corner_speed` +2 and a 2,557–2,559 m drop on most runs with no brake after it, which the hold's conditions do not cover). `tools/patterns.py`, v0.96 → v0.98, identical: 41 reversals, 1 episode 0.3 s (536–546 m), 102 target flips, 99 plan jumps, 34 brake applications (21 short touches, 14 re-applied within 10 steps), 47 shifts (3 undone within 1 s), TC 14.6 s. `tools/width.py` identical (446 m +0.11, 770 m −0.15, 1,528 m −0.41, 2,473 m +0.67, 3,273 m −0.49 at the slowest point). False starts on the single lap, before = after: 6 drop / re-detect pairs above 150 km/h (694 / 705, 710 / 719, 976 / 982, 1,461 / 1,468, 2,630 / 2,658, 2,921 / 2,928 m), 21 wrong-way steer steps, 2 steps with the pedal demand over 0.9 at \|steer\| > 0.5 above 150 km/h (9 with the sent pedal ≥ 0.5). Races used: about 1,340; exploring about 17 min. |
| **Decision** | ❌ Rejected — 0 damage, 0 of 30 off, all-30 73.676 (below 73.688) and no margin spent, but **the gain is inside the noise** (−0.012 s at 1.2 standard errors over the 30 suite runs, +0.007 s over the 40 shifted runs, **−0.001 s over all 70**): the hold changes 13 of 70 runs, 7 faster and 6 slower. It removes the mid-bend drop it was written for, and the laps downstream of it scatter both ways. No follow-up is measured that would make it an enabling change. Driver restored to v0.96. |
| **Learned** | (1) **False-start bend detection is measured out on the plan side as well.** The plan rises during a dropped bend at one of five events and by 2 km/h; an event-keyed hold is ±0.005 s; a fall limit after the re-detection loses 0.3–0.6 s. The car is behind a falling plan through the whole of these zones and the collapse after re-detection is real. With v0.94 (steering side) this closes the item. (2) **The braking-downshift knobs are at their optimum on this car** (`brake_ds_rpm` 16,500 / 18,200, `brake_ds_over` 15 / 28 all slower, the low-`over` and late ones with less flick margin), and a downshift gated on the ABS cut is 0.07 s slower. (3) **A speed gate does not rescue the higher straight-line traction-control limit**: either half of the speed range spends the flick margin, because the cost is the arrival speed at the flick approach (5 km/h more at 2,380 m on the `turn_grip` +0.5 run), not wheelspin at the arc. The low-speed half is faster on 22 of 30 runs: it would pay if the flick approach tolerated the arrival speed. (4) **New tool: section times over the kept suite runs** (`Job(..., keep=csv)` for the 30 runs, then the spread per 100 m and its correlation with the lap) shows where perturbed runs lose time; summed over sections the mean is 0.87 s above the per-section minima, largest at 2,400–2,600 m (0.07 + 0.05 s, not correlated with the lap), 700–800 m (0.06), 1,900–2,000 m (0.06), 2,700–2,800 m (0.05). (5) **Mid-bend drops are a real pattern** (34 in 30 runs; 5 followed by braking and a 3 → 2 downshift mid-bend, 0.03–0.13 s each at 1,500–1,600 m), but removing them moves the mean by nothing measurable: runs differ by ±0.2 s from causes that a single event fix does not reach. (6) At 700–800 m the fast runs take 2.50–2.53 s against 2.58; on the one pair compared (`tc_slip` +0.2 against the base lap) the fast run stays in 3rd to 755 m instead of 740 m and runs 3–5 km/h faster on a wider line (−0.25 against −0.18); not turned into a mechanism. (7) The car brakes falsely at the finish line (pedal up to 0.81 from ~3,608 m, 274 → 267 km/h in 4 steps): it starts at the timing line of a one-lap run, so no measurable cost. |

## v0.99 — Tightened arc below 232 km/h in the sharpness plan (new `arc_tight_v` 232)

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.99 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.99`). One mechanism: **a curve onto a beam that would leave the track at an inner beam is tightened instead of discarded, below `arc_tight_v` 232 km/h.** In the sharpness plan, when the arc onto a beam passes outside the end of a beam nearer the nose, the beam used to be skipped. Now, below 232 km/h, the arc takes the tightest radius that just passes the ends of those inner beams and is planned to that radius and to its shorter chord (2·radius·sin of the beam's bearing); at or above 232 km/h the beam is skipped as before. New knob `arc_tight_v` **232** (line 598); code lines 883–891 (replacing the two-line check at old 882–883). No other value changed. |
| **Why** | Own analysis first, on 70 kept telemetry runs of v0.96 (30 suite runs + 40 shifted-base runs, re-run for pairing: 73.687 / 73.719 / 73.658, all-30 73.688, bm14 73.787, lo05 73.815, identical to the reference). **(1) The flick's arc-entry speed is decided by one chance plan dip.** Speed at 2,365 m over the 70 runs is 226–246 km/h and the flick's \|trackPos\| follows it: where the plan dips at 2,345–2,349 m (allowed 235 / 230, a grip limit on a kink beam, pedal 0.5, 245 → 236 km/h) the car reaches the left arc at 97–99 km/h; where it does not (the six `turn_grip_aero` + and `turn_grip` +0.5 runs: allowed 260 there, full throttle at 2,357–2,361 m at 244 km/h) it reaches it at 101–106, is still 22 km/h over the plan at pedal 0.5 with steer 0.62 at 2,434 m, yaws 20° instead of 6° in the arc, slides to 0.76–0.88 and drops to 54–57 km/h instead of 62–66: **those runs are also 0.2 s slower through 2,300–2,600 m** (8.62–8.66 s against 8.40–8.46). From 2,365 m on the plan falls 50 km/h in 16 m (about 55 m/s²) against a car that slows at 28, so every run is 20–27 km/h over the plan with the pedal ABS-cut from 2,394 m to the arc; nothing in the car's own signals separates the hot runs before 2,365 m. **(2) The start kink is a second chance event.** 200–300 m takes 1.430 s on 39 of the 70 runs and 1.452–1.606 s on 31 (11 of the 30 suite runs; mean loss 0.033 s, correlation with the lap +0.47, and the 20 km/h deficit is carried to the 450 m braking). Recomputing the sharpness plan per beam from the telemetry (matches the logged allowed speed to 0.5 km/h): through the whole kink the binding limit is **the braking distance to `corner_speed` on the −7° beam** (65–96 m → 194–247 km/h; its grip limit is 250–330), and the car passes flat only when its position happens to make that beam 84–96 m long; on the slow runs the −12° beam (103–126 m, 233–276 km/h) is skipped for a 0–5 m shortfall at the −7° beam at 184–190 m (allowed 265 → 206 → 247 → 194 km/h step to step; pedal 0.67 and 0.49; 219 → 204 km/h). **Candidates tried (paired against the re-run v0.96; all-30 base 73.688):** (a) **plan hold on a committed approach** (direction A; allowed speed may rise only 1 km/h per step while over 200 km/h with less than `T` s of road in view): T 0.85 / 1.0 / 1.2 s → all-30 73.739 / 73.942 / 73.890, suite max 0.881 / 0.918 / 1 of 30 off: slower **and wider**, because the hold turns the brake touch at 2,333 m into a lift, the yaw changes and the 2,345 m dip does not come (2,363 m at 242 instead of 235 km/h); (b) **weaker ABS cut when far behind the plan with a straight wheel** (direction A, braking feedback; cut to 0.65 / 0.8 / 1.0 of the pedal instead of 0.5 while more than 20 km/h over the plan and \|steer\| < 0.3): 73.730 / 73.786 / 73.798, suite max 0.777 / 0.823 / 0.829; 25 km/h over: 73.734; \|steer\| < 0.15: 73.717, max 0.781; the time is lost in 2,400–2,500 m (+0.02 to +0.065 s), flick \|trackPos\| −0.010 at best; (c) **arc end speed** (new: a beam the car curves onto is planned to end at a share of that arc's grip speed instead of `corner_speed`): share 0.3 leaves the track at 457 m; gated on a slanted edge between neighbouring beam ends (< 26–30°): off at 467 m / 1,103 m; < 20°: damage at 2,351 m; < 15°: off at the flick. The `corner_speed` end-of-beam assumption is what protects every real corner and the flick approach; (d) **tightened arc, ungated** (the banked idea, now measured on all 70): all-30 73.733 (paired +0.045 s), shifted 73.783 (−0.018 s), all 70 +0.009 s (SE 0.021), bm14 on-track max **0.998** (`turn_grip_aero` +, 2,463 m); it removes the start-kink loss on 12 of the 31 slow runs (200–400 m −0.027 s) and pays +0.015 s at 400–600 m and +0.020 s at 2,300–2,600 m; only where the shortfall is under 5 % / 15 %: 73.744 / 73.754, bm14 max 0.989 / 0.988; (e) **tightened arc below a speed** (this version): below 225 / 232 / 240 km/h → all-30 73.709 / 73.733 / 73.772, all 70 −0.009 / −0.012 / +0.035 s, suite max 0.855 / 0.794 / 0.900, shifted max 0.894 / 0.897 / 0.938; 195–232 km/h only: all 70 −0.001 s, max 0.939. Direction B (the set-up's side signal used for the speed plan or the turn-in point) was not trialled: the time went into the two chance events above. |
| **Prediction** | −0.02 to −0.03 s over the 70 runs from the start kink (31 slow runs, 0.033 s mean), nothing at the flick because the approach runs above 232 km/h until 2,369 m. |
| **Lap time** | 1:13.63 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 285 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 56 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run `runs/run_20261003_181023.csv`; TORCS time 73.638 s → on screen 1:13:63 (v0.96: 73.590; +0.048 s: 2,500–2,600 m +0.140, 2,400–2,500 m −0.060). Max \|trackPos\| 0.748 at ~2,477 m (flick; v0.96 0.679; hairpin 0.56); slowest corner 56 km/h at ~3,274 m. **Suites on the edited driver** (v0.96 in brackets): 73.696 / 73.762 / 73.740 (73.687 / 73.719 / 73.658), **all-30 73.733** (73.688), max 0.794 / 0.783 / 0.753 (0.761 / 0.789 / 0.749; `corner_speed` +2, flick 2,477 m), **0 of 30 off**. **Shifted bases 0 of 40 off**: bm14 73.742 (73.787), on-track max 0.897 (`brake_margin` −2, hairpin exit 3,278 m; was 0.893); lo05 73.752 (73.815), max 0.863 (`brake_margin` −2, flick 2,476 m; was 0.876). **Paired per run against v0.96:** 30 suite runs **+0.045 s** (SE 0.022, 11 of 30 faster); 40 shifted runs −0.054 s (SE 0.028, 22 of 40 faster); **all 70 −0.012 s (SE 0.019, 33 of 70 faster)**. The start kink is not fixed by this gate: 41 of 70 runs take more than 1.45 s in 200–300 m (31 on v0.96; mean 1.464 against 1.463 s); the slow runs brake there at 205–232 km/h, around the gate speed (read as the gate switching the tightened arc on and off; not traced); ungated or below 240 km/h it is 19 of 70. Section means over the 70 runs: 400–500 m +0.010, 2,500–2,600 m +0.009, 500–600 / 700–800 / 2,700–2,800 / 2,900–3,000 m −0.005 to −0.007. `tools/patterns.py` (v0.96 → v0.99): steering reversals 41 → 51, 1 episode 0.3 s (536–545 m, unchanged), target flips 102 → 104, plan jumps 99 → 89, brake applications 34 → 35 (short touches 21 → 23, re-applied within 10 steps 14 → 16), shifts 47 → 49 (undone within 1 s 3 → 8; not traced), TC 14.6 → 14.5 s. `tools/width.py`: 446 m +0.11 → +0.12 at its slowest point, 770 m −0.15 → −0.09, 1,528 m −0.41 → −0.45, 1,927 m entry +0.25 → +0.30; the rest unchanged. |
| **Decision** | ❌ Rejected — 0 damage, 0 of 30 off and shifted bases 0 of 40 off, but **all-30 is 73.733, above v0.96's 73.688** (paired +0.045 s at 2 standard errors), the −0.012 s over all 70 runs is inside the noise (0.6 standard errors), the control patterns are a little worse (reversals 41 → 51, undone shifts 3 → 8) and the start kink it was written for is not fixed at this gate. No margin gained, so it is not an enabling change either. Driver restored to v0.96 (`git checkout v0.96 -- snakeoil3_v1.py`). |
| **Learned** | (1) **The flick margin is set by whether one plan dip at 2,345–2,349 m occurs**, and that dip is the kink's grip limit, not a braking limit: runs where the plan's grip is higher (`turn_grip_aero` +, `turn_grip` +0.5) or where the yaw differs by a few hundredths of a radian skip it, arrive 7–10 km/h faster at 2,365 m and 3–7 km/h faster at the arc, and are both the widest and 0.2 s slower through the flick. That is why everything that raises the plan or the arrival speed (looser TC, higher `brake_max`, tightened arc) is paid there. (2) **Holding the plan down makes it worse, not better** (three settings): the flick approach depends on brake touches at exact places, and a lift in place of a touch moves the car enough to lose the next dip. A robust flick approach needs braking that does not depend on the kink's grip limit, i.e. information about the slow corner behind the kink, which the beams give only from 2,376 m (road ahead 82 m at 227 km/h: 27 m/s² needed at once). (3) **More brake when behind the plan loses time in 2,400–2,500 m** on the normal runs: the approach is not short of braking on those, only on the hot ones, and no signal before 2,365 m tells them apart. (4) **The start kink is limited by the braking distance to `corner_speed` on the −7° beam, never by grip**; 31 of 70 runs lift or brake there and lose 0.02–0.18 s each (0.033 s mean, more on the `line_offset` 0.5 base: 9 of 20 runs at 1.50 s or more). (5) **The end-of-beam `corner_speed` assumption cannot be relaxed by geometry**: an arc-based end speed or an edge-slant test opens the plan at 457 m, 1,103 m and the flick approach before it opens the start kink. (6) **The tightened arc is closed**: measured on all 70 runs it is +0.009 s ungated and −0.012 s at the best gate, with all-30 +0.02 to +0.08 s in every form and the bm14 margin at 0.94–1.00 unless gated to 232 km/h or lower; its start-kink gain (200–400 m −0.027 s) is given back at the 450 m braking and the flick, as v0.93 found. (7) all-30 and the 40 shifted runs disagree in sign for this change (+0.045 against −0.054 s): judge on all 70. (8) Races used: about 1,260 in 19 minutes of exploring. |

## v1.00 — Corner set-up as a planned lane change: out, then a soft trail-in (new `lc_on` 1 / `lc_out` 0.6 / `lc_gain` 1.0 / `lc_cap` 0.3 / `lc_road` 70 / `lc_in` 0.5 / `lc_tgain` 1.0 / `lc_tcap` 0.15 / `lc_n` 80)

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v1.00 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v1.00`). One mechanism: **the corner set-up's side signal drives a planned lane change in two phases.** (1) Outward phase: while the set-up is held, the car is pulled to `lc_out` 0.6 on the outside with `lc_gain` 1.0 steer per unit of trackPos, capped at `lc_cap` 0.3 (v0.96: `setup_offset` 0.85 at `line_gain` 0.5 capped at `setup_pull` 0.15), until the road along the track direction falls to `lc_road` 70 m (v0.96: `setup_road` 85). (2) Trail-in (new): below 70 m of road the set-up's side is remembered (`c.trail_side`) and, until the bend is detected (or for `lc_n` 80 steps at most), the car is pulled toward `lc_in` 0.5 on the inside with `lc_tgain` 1.0 capped at `lc_tcap` 0.15: a soft early turn-in ahead of the unchanged step at bend detection. New knobs at lines 568–576 (`lc_on` 1 = on; 0 reproduces v0.96 exactly: all-30 73.688); the set-up's road window at line 695; the lane-change code at lines 702–720 (the v0.96 pull, old 693–695, is now the `lc_on` 0 branch at 721–723). Old lines 568–692 moved +9, old ≥ 693 moved +28. No other value changed. |
| **Why** | The assignment was direction B, the racing line with earlier information (the user's priority). Own analysis first, on the v0.96 run: on every approach the set-up moves the car only 0.2–0.25 of trackPos (270–350 m: +0.02 → −0.24; 870–940 m: +0.29 → +0.43) and from 85 m of road to the bend detection (45–50 m of road) nothing acts on the line; in the medium bends the car's speed equals the allowed speed with 35–40 m of road in view (1,039 m: 156 km/h allowed 153 at 38 m; 1,530 m: 139 / 140 at 36 m; 2,988 m: 142 / 142 at 35 m). **Inherited conclusion challenged: "a car that really is on the outside before turn-in already earns more allowed speed". It does not.** **Forced-wide-entry diagnostic** (position-gated, never the version: in a window ending at the bend detection the set-up is replaced by a pull to the outside at gain 1.0 capped at 0.4; the car reaches +0.40 to +0.61 of the outside at −80 m, against +0.09 to +0.37). Single laps (base 73.590), target 0.6 / 0.8: 446 m +0.164 / +0.086 s, 770 m +0.226 / +0.134, 1,042 m +0.342 / +0.429, 1,528 m +0.146 / +0.100, 1,927 m +0.188 / +0.088, 2,988 m +0.146 / +0.284; 0 off. Suite 1 (base 73.687), target 0.6: 446 m 73.752 (+0.065), 770 m 73.934 (+0.247), 1,042 m 73.954 (+0.267), 1,528 m 73.789 (+0.102), 1,927 m 73.797 (+0.111), 2,988 m 73.774 (+0.087): **a wide entry alone is slower at every bend.** Step trace at 1,042 m: the turn-in comes at the same place (985 m) from +0.44 instead of +0.36, the fronts saturate (steer −0.55), the car never reaches the inside (slowest point at +0.13 of the outside instead of −0.46) and the plan allows 134–140 km/h there instead of 154. **Candidates (my own; the second and third are not in the plan or the changelog):** (a) the wide entry by lane change (above); (b) **wide entry plus an early soft trail-in** (from 20 / 40 / 60 m before the detection point a capped pull 0.15 / 0.30 toward the inside): single laps −0.03 to +0.33 s, best at 20 m and cap 0.15; suite 1 with 20 m / 0.15: 446 m 73.705 (+0.018), 770 m 73.913 (+0.227), 1,042 m 73.712 (+0.025), 1,528 m 73.626 (−0.061), 1,927 m 73.651 (−0.035), 2,988 m 73.663 (−0.023), suite max up to 0.917 (1,927 m) and 0.907 (1,042 m) against 0.761; the trail-in without the wide entry (v0.96 set-up kept), single laps: −0.026, −0.006, +0.020, +0.170, +0.042, +0.142 s; (c) **wide entry, trail-in and +5 km/h on the plan through that bend** (position-gated, as the follow-up test for an enabling change), suite 1: 446 m 73.784 (+0.097), 770 m 73.959 (+0.272), 1,042 m 73.660 (−0.027), 1,528 m 73.617 (−0.070), 1,927 m 73.580 (−0.106), 2,988 m 73.655 (−0.032); the +5 km/h alone: +0.100, +0.005, +0.041, −0.040, −0.071, +0.032. So position-gated, three bends (1,528, 1,927, 2,988 m) gain 0.02–0.06 s each from the out-and-in line and 446 m, 770 m and 1,042 m lose. **Without position gates** (this version's mechanism, all-30, base 73.688): v0.96 set-up plus the trail-in below 85 m: 74.349, max 0.955 (hairpin exit, `brake_margin` −2); v0.96 set-up plus the trail-in below 70 m: 26 of 30 off at the flick (2,468–2,476 m); out 0.6 / cap 0.4 / road 70: 74.251, max 0.789; out 0.6 / cap 0.3 / road 70 (**this version**): 74.102, max 0.764; out 0.5 / cap 0.2: 73.815 but 1 of 30 off (`brake_gain` +0.01, flick 2,477 m); trail-in cap 0.08: 74.131, max 0.803; road 60: 74.328, max 0.823; no trail-in (`lc_n` 0): 74.223, max 0.815. No speed, steer or sight gate separates the bends where it gains from those where it loses (446 m and 1,927 m are both approached at 268 km/h with the same beam picture; the flick approach at 275). |
| **Prediction** | From the position-gated diagnostic: about −0.1 s if the three gaining bends could be kept and the others left alone; without gates a loss, because 446 m, 770 m and the flick approach take the same lane change. |
| **Lap time** | 1:13.94 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 284 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 54 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run `runs/run_20261003_182458.csv`; TORCS time 73.942 s → on screen 1:13:94 (v0.96: 73.590; +0.352 s, of which 400–600 m +0.20, 2,400–2,600 m +0.14, 1,000–1,200 m +0.04, 1,400–1,600 m −0.05). Max \|trackPos\| 0.669 at ~2,476 m (flick; v0.96 0.679); slowest corner 54 km/h at ~3,273 m. **Suites on this driver** (v0.96 in brackets): 73.872 / 74.135 / 74.297 (73.687 / 73.719 / 73.658), **all-30 74.102** (73.688), max 0.738 / 0.764 / 0.741 (0.761 / 0.789 / 0.749; `upshift_rpm` −200, flick 2,474 m), **0 of 30 off**. **Shifted bases 0 of 40 off**: bm14 74.006 (73.787), max 0.872 (`brake_aero` +, flick left arc 2,462 m; was 0.893 at the hairpin exit); lo05 74.003 (73.815), max 0.767 (`brake_margin` −2, flick 2,473 m; was 0.876). **Paired per run against v0.96** (base re-run: 73.688, bm14 73.787, lo05 73.815): 30 suite runs **+0.414 s** (SE 0.053, 2 of 30 faster); 40 shifted runs +0.204 s (SE 0.042, 9 of 40 faster); **all 70 +0.294 s (SE 0.035, 11 of 70 faster)**. `tools/width.py` (v0.96 → v1.00; entry at −80 m / slowest point / exit at +80 m, + = outside): 446 m +0.21 / +0.11 / +0.36 → +0.33 / **+0.42** / +0.33 at 91 km/h (was 101); 770 m +0.21 / −0.15 / +0.30 → +0.21 / −0.16 / +0.44; 1,042 m +0.37 / −0.46 / +0.19 → +0.49 / −0.26 / +0.28 at 149 (was 154); 1,528 m +0.09 / −0.41 / −0.02 → +0.23 / −0.47 / −0.08; 1,927 m +0.25 / −0.14 / +0.10 → +0.32 / −0.21 / +0.05; 2,988 m +0.14 / −0.46 / +0.03 → +0.27 / −0.41 / +0.08; hairpin unchanged. `tools/patterns.py` (v0.96 → v1.00): steering reversals 41 → **122**, episodes 1 (0.3 s) → **4 (2.2 s)**: 279–333 m (0.75 s, 11 reversals at 261 km/h, the target switching between −0.6, −0.47 and 0), 537–551 m (as before), 1,106–1,143 m (0.64 s, 211 km/h) and 2,847–2,876 m (0.44 s, 234 km/h); target flips 102 → 150; plan jumps 99 → 108; brake applications 34 → 32 (short touches 21 → 18, re-applied within 10 steps 14 → 18); shifts 47 → 45 (3 undone, unchanged); TC 14.6 → 14.8 s. |
| **Decision** | ❌ Rejected — 0 damage, 0 of 30 off and shifted bases 0 of 40 off, but all-30 is 74.102 against 73.688 (paired +0.414 s; all 70 runs +0.294 s at 8 standard errors), the stronger pull brings a steering oscillation on three approaches (reversals 41 → 122, 2.2 s in episodes), and although the entries moved out (+0.07 to +0.14 at five bends) the slowest points did not move in (446 m and 1,042 m moved out and are slower). Not an enabling change: the only follow-up measured that pays (plan +5 km/h on the out-and-in line at 1,528 / 1,927 / 2,988 m) needs to know which bend is coming. Driver restored to v0.96 (`git checkout v0.96 -- snakeoil3_v1.py`). |
| **Learned** | (1) **A wide entry is worth nothing on this car by itself: forced to +0.4 … +0.6 of the outside at −80 m, every medium bend is 0.07–0.27 s slower on suite 1.** The turn-in still comes at the bend detection, the fronts are already saturated there, so the car runs the same arc shifted outward, misses the inside and the plan gives it less speed (1,042 m: 134–140 km/h on the outside half against 154 on the inside). (2) **The corner speed of the medium bends follows the plan, not the line**: with the wide entry and a trail-in the car reaches the same inside point and the slowest speed is the same 153–154 km/h at 1,042 m; the car rides the allowed speed with 35–40 m of road in view (read from the speed / allowed / road columns; not recomputed per beam). A wider line pays only where the plan is then raised (+5 km/h on the out-and-in line: 1,927 m −0.106 s, 1,528 m −0.070 s on suite 1; +5 km/h alone: −0.071 / −0.040 s there, which says those two bends have plan headroom anyway: a non-position-gated form of that is the open item). (3) **The out-and-in line needs an earlier turn-in to be even neutral**: a capped pull of 0.15 toward the inside from about 20 m before the detection point (road in view 60–75 m) turns +0.07 … +0.27 s into −0.06 … +0.03 s at five of six bends (three gain: 1,528 m, 1,927 m, 2,988 m; 446 m and 1,042 m are flat at +0.02 … +0.03 s; 770 m stays +0.23 s); 40–60 m earlier or a cap of 0.30 is 0.1–0.3 s slower (early apex). (4) **Per bend the sign differs**: 1,528 m, 1,927 m and 2,988 m gain 0.02–0.06 s, 446 m and 1,042 m are flat, 770 m loses 0.23 s (it is entered from a right-hand curve at 600–690 m, not a straight). (5) **Without position gates the mechanism loses 0.4–0.7 s or leaves the track at the flick**: the time goes at 400–600 m (+0.20 s: the 446 m bend's slowest point moves out to +0.42 at 91 km/h) and at the flick (+0.14 s: on the approach at 2,283–2,315 m the set-up reads a right-hand bend, pulls the car to +0.6 and then trails in the wrong way before the kink); nothing in speed, steer or the beams separates those approaches from the three that gain, so this is the same wall as before: it needs to know which corner is coming (track memory, on hold). (6) A stronger set-up pull (gain 1.0, cap 0.3–0.4) oscillates against the heading term at 210–260 km/h (0.4–0.75 s episodes): any future lane change should command a heading (trackPos rate), not a larger proportional pull. (7) Races used: about 720 in 25 minutes of exploring. |
