## v1.01 — Brake dab keeps the stored throttle (new `dab_n` 2 / `dab_keep` 0.7 / `dab_steer` 0.15 / `dab_v` 230)

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v1.01 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v1.01`). One mechanism: **the first steps of a brake application keep the stored throttle when the wheel is near straight and the car is below 230 km/h.** Until now any brake pedal of `touch_brake` 0.15 or more zeroed the stored throttle, which then climbed back from its floor at +0.05 per step. Now, for the first `dab_n` 2 steps of a brake application, whatever the pedal, `dab_keep` 0.7 of the stored throttle is kept per step, while the smoothed \|steer\| (`c.steer_f`) is below `dab_steer` 0.15 and the speed is below `dab_v` 230 km/h; from the third step, or with the wheel turned, or at speed, the old rule applies. Nothing is sent to the engine while braking, as before. New knobs at lines 615–618 (`dab_n` 0 = off, reproduces v0.96 exactly: all-30 73.688); code at lines 932, 941–954, 957 and 1056–1057 (`c.brk_n` counts the steps of the current brake application). Old lines 615–927 moved +4, 928–935 +5, 936–938 +18, 939–1036 +19, old ≥ 1037 +20. No other value changed. |
| **Why** | Own analysis first, on the v0.96 run and on 70 kept telemetry runs of v0.96 (30 suite runs + 40 shifted-base runs, re-run for pairing: 73.687 / 73.719 / 73.658, all-30 73.688, bm14 73.787, lo05 73.815, identical to the reference). **(1) Lead 1, plan headroom per bend: the limit signals do not separate the bends.** At the slowest point (±10 m) of the six medium bends on the v0.96 lap (speed / allowed speed km/h, mean \|steer\|, slip angle): 446 m 103.8 / 104.9, 0.61, 5.4° (8.0° just after); 770 m 120.9 / 122.5, 0.51, 6.1°; 1,042 m 156.5 / 160.0, 0.25, 3.1°; 1,528 m 140.4 / 140.1, 0.36, 4.0°; 1,927 m 120.9 / 123.4, 0.46, 3.4°; 2,988 m 144.1 / 145.9, 0.32, 3.8°. The two bends where v1.00's position-gated +5 km/h gained (1,927 m, 1,528 m) and the two where it lost (1,042 m, 2,988 m) overlap in steer (0.46 / 0.36 against 0.25 / 0.32) and in slip (3.4° / 4.0° against 3.1° / 3.8°); only 446 m and 770 m stand apart (slip 5.4–8°, steer 0.5–0.6). 1,927 m differs in one thing: the car is 14–18 km/h over the plan under an ABS-cut brake until 1,919 m and rides the plan for only 13 m (1,921–1,934 m). **(2) Section times over the 70 runs** (mean lap 73.703 s, sum of per-section minima 72.526): the one section with a large chance loss is 200–300 m (median 1.430 s, 18 of 70 runs above 1.47 s, mean loss 0.033 s); the other tails are 0.008–0.022 s each. **Inherited conclusion challenged: "a faster arrival at the 450 m braking is worth nothing" (v0.93, repeated in v0.99).** On the 70 v0.96 runs the 18 kink-slow runs are +0.028 s in 100–200 m, +0.107 in 200–300, +0.028 in 300–400, −0.011 in 400–500 and +0.010 in 500–700 m: +0.161 s over 100–700 m and +0.169 s on the lap. The kink loss is kept to the finish line; the tightened arc gave it back because it also changed the plan at 450 m and at the flick. **(3) Where the kink loss comes from** (step trace, `line_offset` 0.5 base, 160–190 m; the events are at 160–190 m, the 200–300 m section only shows the deficit): the −12° beam opens at 164 m (35 → 103 m), the bend is detected, the steering steps 0 → +0.20 → +0.34 at 222 km/h, the nose yaws 7° left and the −12° beam closes on the inside edge again (35 m); the plan falls back to the braking distance on the −7° beam (allowed 233 → 214 → 220 km/h) and the car, 9 km/h over, brakes for **one step** at pedal 0.47. That step takes about 1 km/h; but it zeroes the stored throttle, which restarts at 0.20 and needs 16 steps to reach 1.0: 218.9 km/h at 175 m against 227.5 on a run whose plan stayed 0.8 km/h above its speed at 159 m. A second dip at 183–190 m (pedal 0.88 / 0.96 for two steps) takes it to 204 km/h, and the 20–25 km/h deficit is carried to the 450 m braking. **Candidates (all my own; A, C and D are not in the plan or the changelog), paired against the re-run v0.96:** (A) **slip-keyed grip to spare** (lead 1 as a mechanism: the sharpness plan's `grip_boost` also given, by a share, while the slip angle is small, whatever the steering): share 0.3 / 0.6 / 1.0 with none from 5° (fade 2°) → all-30 73.735 / 73.728 / 73.759, max 0.775 / 0.903 / 0.879; none from 4°: share 0.6 / 1.0 → 73.697 / 73.764, max 0.860 / 0.904; none from 6° (fade 3°): 73.795; every form slower (+0.010 to +0.107 s paired) and the margin goes at the flick's left arc (2,461–2,462 m). (B) **one-step plan-dip delay** (a fall of the allowed speed larger than J km/h in one step is taken one step late): J 10 / 20 / 40 below 240 km/h → 73.739 / 73.730 / 73.702 (max 0.797 / 0.874 / 0.872); J 20 at any speed 73.723; with the wheel straight 73.717–73.727: slower in every form, as every plan filter before it. (C) **a kink beam at ±9.5°** in place of the ±1.7° or the ±2.5° beam (the look-ahead bearing kept on the old angles by interpolation; with the old angles the variant reproduces 73.688): 74.058 with 4 of 30 off / 74.153 with 3 of 30 off (flick, 2,468–2,480 m); single lap 74.004, slower at every bend (+0.04 to +0.12 s each): the extra beam changes the leave-the-track check and the credit in every bend, so it would need the plan's grip re-tuned. (D) **brake dab** (this version). Without a speed gate, all-30: `dab_n` 1 / 2 / 3 at keep 1.0 → 73.728 / 73.703 / 73.697, max 0.920 / 0.935 / 0.970 (flick left arc 2,462 m); `dab_steer` 0.3 → 73.696 (0.935); no steer gate → 73.724 with 1 of 30 off (`turn_grip_aero` +, 2,477 m); keep 0.7 → 73.684 (0.878). With a speed gate, all 70 paired: below 240 km/h, `dab_n` 2, keep 1.0 → −0.008 s (SE 0.014), all-30 73.699; keep 0.7 → −0.023 s (SE 0.010), all-30 73.676; keep 0.5 → −0.011 s (SE 0.011), 73.687; `dab_n` 1 / 3 at keep 0.7 → −0.023 / −0.023 s (the same runs); `dab_n` 3, keep 1.0 → −0.007 s; `dab_n` 3, keep 1.0, `dab_steer` 0.3 → −0.032 s (SE 0.020, 31 of 70 faster, all-30 73.696, bm14 73.742, lo05 73.737: larger but noisier, not chosen); below 250 km/h → −0.014 s (SE 0.013), all-30 73.688, **shifted max 0.980** (lo05, `turn_grip_aero` +, flick left arc 2,463 m) and 0.905 (bm14): the gate has a cliff at 250, where it reaches the brake touches on the flick approach; **below 230 km/h (this version) → −0.024 s (SE 0.010)**, margins unchanged. Not measured (race budget): `dab_v` below 230, `dab_steer` 0.3 at keep 0.7. |
| **Prediction** | About −0.03 s over the 70 runs if every one-step dab at the start kink kept its throttle (18 slow runs × 0.16 s / 70 = 0.041 s at most); nothing on the unperturbed lap, which passes the kink without a dab. |
| **Lap time** | 1:13.59 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 285 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 55 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run `runs/run_20261003_184707.csv`; TORCS time 73.590 s → on screen 1:13:59, **the same lap as v0.96**: 3,605 rows, identical except the logged stored throttle on 9 braking rows (361, 692, 1,869–1,870, 2,640–2,641, 2,944–2,945 and 3,195 m; at five of them it reads −0.007 / −0.012: the dab keeps 0.7 of a stored throttle that was already at −0.01; nothing is sent while braking and the driving is unchanged). Max \|trackPos\| 0.679 at ~2,476 m (flick; hairpin 0.56); slowest corner 55 km/h at ~3,273 m. Rerun byte-identical. **Suites on the committed driver** (v0.96 in brackets): 73.660 / 73.711 / 73.658 (73.687 / 73.719 / 73.658), **all-30 73.676** (73.688), max 0.761 / 0.789 / 0.749 (unchanged; `brake_margin` −2, flick 2,475 m), **0 of 30 off**. **Shifted bases 0 of 40 off**: bm14 73.773 (73.787), on-track max 0.893 (`brake_margin` −2, hairpin exit 3,278 m; unchanged); lo05 73.762 (73.815), max 0.810 (`turn_grip_aero` +, flick left arc 2,462 m; was 0.876 on the same run, which is one of the three slower runs: a knock-on, not a margin gain to count on). **Paired per run against v0.96:** 30 suite runs **−0.011 s** (SE 0.008; 4 faster, 1 slower, 25 identical); 40 shifted runs −0.034 s (SE 0.016; 11 faster, 2 slower, 27 identical); **all 70 −0.024 s (SE 0.010; 15 faster, 3 slower, 52 identical)**. The 15 faster runs gain 0.026–0.344 s; the three slower lose 0.040, 0.070 and 0.300 s (lo05 with `turn_grip_aero` −: faster to 400 m, then +0.16 s at 2,700–2,900 m, a knock-on). Of the 18 changed runs 16 first differ from v0.96 at 160–190 m (the start kink), one at 2,370 m and one at 2,630 m. Section means over the 70 runs: 100–200 m −0.003, 200–300 m −0.010 (1.463 → 1.453 s; runs above 1.47 s 18 → 14), 300–400 m −0.003, 2,500–2,600 m −0.005; 100–700 m 12.338 → 12.324 s. The second kink dip (183–190 m: two braking steps at pedal 0.5–0.96 with the smoothed \|steer\| at about 0.15) still restarts the throttle on the traced run (lo05 base, unperturbed: 224.7 → 214.6 km/h at 183–191 m). `tools/patterns.py` (v0.96 → v1.01, single lap): unchanged (41 reversals, 1 episode 0.3 s at 536–546 m, 102 target flips, 99 plan jumps, 34 brake applications of which 21 short and 14 re-applied within 10 steps, 47 shifts with 3 undone, TC 14.6 s). `tools/width.py`: unchanged (446 m +0.21 / +0.11 / +0.36; 770 m +0.21 / −0.15 / +0.30; 1,042 m +0.37 / −0.46 / +0.19; 1,528 m +0.09 / −0.41 / −0.02; 1,927 m +0.25 / −0.14 / +0.10; 2,988 m +0.14 / −0.46 / +0.03). |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — 0 damage, 0 of 30 off, shifted bases 0 of 40 off, all-30 73.676 against 73.688, **−0.024 s over all 70 runs at 2.4 standard errors (15 faster, 3 slower, 52 untouched)**, and no margin spent (suite max 0.789 and bm14 max 0.893 unchanged). A small gain, but a targeted one: the change acts where it was aimed (16 of 18 changed runs at the start kink) and leaves the unperturbed lap and 52 of 70 runs exactly as they were. The best single lap stays 1:13.59. |
| **Learned** | (1) **A faster arrival at the 450 m braking is worth its full time**: on v0.96's own 70 runs the kink-slow runs are 0.161 s slower over 100–700 m and 0.169 s on the lap; the tightened arc (v0.93, v0.96, v0.99) lost its kink gain because it also changed the plan at 450 m and at the flick, not because the speed was worthless. (2) **Most of the start-kink loss was the throttle restart, not the brake**: a one-step brake at pedal 0.47 takes ~1 km/h, the stored throttle restarting from 0.20 at +0.05 per step takes 8–9 km/h by 175 m. Keeping 0.7 of the stored throttle through the first braking steps recovers −0.024 s over 70 runs; keeping all of it (1.0) or half (0.5) gains less (−0.008 / −0.011 s). (3) **The dab rule must stay below ~240 km/h and near a straight wheel**: below 250 km/h it reaches the flick approach's brake touches (shifted max 0.980), without a speed gate the flick's left arc runs to 0.92–0.97, without a steer gate 1 of 30 leaves the track. (4) **The kink is only partly fixed** (slow runs 18 → 14 of 70): the second dip at 183–190 m brakes for two steps with the smoothed \|steer\| near the 0.15 gate; `dab_steer` 0.3 with `dab_n` 3 and keep 1.0 measured −0.032 s (SE 0.020) over 70 runs, so `dab_steer` 0.2–0.3 at keep 0.7 is the open follow-up (worth about 0.01–0.02 s). (5) **Lead 1 is closed in a sensed form**: the bends with plan headroom and those without show the same steer and slip at the limit (0.25–0.46, 3.1–4.0°); a grip boost keyed to a small slip angle is 0.01–0.11 s slower in six settings and spends the flick margin. (6) **One-step plan-dip delays lose like every plan filter** (+0.015 to +0.051 s). (7) **An extra beam at ±9.5° is not a free sensor**: it would see through the kink, but in place of the ±1.7° or ±2.5° beam it is 0.38–0.46 s slower with 3–4 of 30 off at the flick, because the sharpness plan's credit and leave-the-track check change in every bend; it would need the plan re-tuned around it (not done). (8) The cause of the kink dip itself is the turn-in overshoot at bend detection (steer +0.34 at 222 km/h when the −12° beam opens, after which that beam closes again): the same d²-weighted bearing jump as at 394–407 m. (9) Races used: about 1,495 in 19 minutes of exploring. |

## v1.02 — Bend-drop debounce (new `drop_n` 4 / `drop_held` 30 / `drop_jump` 0.5 / `drop_v` 140)

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v1.02 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v1.02`; **rejected, the driver at HEAD is restored to v1.01**). One mechanism: **bend-drop debounce.** A bend ends when the look-ahead bearing falls below `line_aim_off` (1°). Now an established bend (held at least `drop_held` 30 steps) is not dropped for up to `drop_n` 4 steps when the \|bearing\| fell by more than `drop_jump` 0.5° in one step, above `drop_v` 140 km/h; if the bearing is back above `line_aim_off` within those steps the bend simply continues, otherwise it is dropped as before. New knobs at lines 619–622 (`drop_n` 0 = off, reproduces v1.01 exactly in all 70 runs); code at lines 668–684 (`c.drop_k` counts the held steps, `c.side_n` the steps the bend has been held, `c.aim_prev` the previous bearing). In the tagged driver v1.01's lines 619–664 moved +4 and lines ≥ 666 moved +19. No other value changed. |

Mean lap minus the sum of per-section minima: 1.16 s (the lap clock has 0.02 s steps, so tails under ~0.01 s are at the resolution). **(2) Lead 3, finish-line braking: nothing to gain.** On 53 of the 70 runs the car lifts or brakes before the line, but only on the last one or two logged rows (earliest 3,606.4 m, 274 km/h, pedal 0–0.62; the plan sees the first kink across the line, allowed 262–268 km/h): under 0.001 s. **(3) A clean event behind a tail: the −12° beam grazing the inside edge mid-bend.** At 2,714–2,751 m (left-hand bend, 163–171 km/h, bend held 67–105 steps) on 14 of 70 runs the −12° beam falls from 62–69 m to 17–18 m for 2–3 steps and comes back (73 m); the distance-squared bearing collapses (−1.65° → +0.04°), the bend is dropped, and on 6 of the 14 the bearing returns only to −1.4 … −1.85°: above `line_aim_off` but under the 2° needed to detect a bend, so the line target is gone for the rest of the bend (steering +0.24 → −0.22 in three steps). The 14 runs are 0.072 s slower over 2,700–2,900 m than the other 56 (0.014 s over all 70). The same beam closing is behind the one +0.42 s loss at 1,000–1,200 m (bend dropped at 1,026 m at 156 km/h, steering −0.06 → +0.34, plan 166 → 130 km/h, brake 0.71, 3 → 2, 125 instead of 155 km/h). **Inherited conclusion challenged:** v0.98 found that fixing mid-bend drops moves nothing, but its hold needed the bearing to stay > 0.5° on the bend's side, which these collapses (to ~0°) fail; so the question was open for this kind of drop. Measured here: it moves nothing either (below). **Candidates, all paired against the re-run v1.01 over the 70 runs** (mean, SE; all-30): (A) lead 1, the dab's own neighbours: `dab_steer` 0.20 / 0.25 / 0.30 → +0.001 (0.004) / +0.004 (0.006) / +0.004 (0.007); 73.671 / 73.683 / 73.683; `dab_v` 220 → +0.014 (0.009), 73.686, lo05 max back at 0.876: v1.01's values are the optimum. (B) own, new: **softer steering step when flat-out** (the root cause of the kink dip: steering step limited to 0.10 / 0.05 per step above 200 km/h with the stored throttle at 1 and no brake): +0.066 (0.023) / +0.177 (0.022); 73.775 / 73.886; shifted max 0.975 (lo05, `turn_grip_aero` +, 2,464 m) at 0.10: the turn-in step is needed even in the flat-out form. (C) own, new: **stored throttle restored after a short straight-line brake** (to its value before a brake application of ≤ 3 / ≤ 6 steps, same gates as the dab): +0.003 (0.005) / +0.000 (0.007); 73.685 / 73.690; bm14 max 0.933 at 6. On the traced kink run the loss is now the brake itself (four steps at pedal 0.44–0.57 at 183–187 m take 224.7 → 216.6 km/h), not the restart. (D) own, new: `lock_gear_v` 112 / 120 (hypothesis: the hot flick arrivals miss first gear at full lock): identical to the base in all 70 runs; every run reaches full lock at 82–96 km/h and takes 1st. (E) own, new: **bend-drop debounce** (this version). Ungated, bends held ≥ 10 steps, 4 steps: +0.062 (0.017), 73.767; by section 2,700–2,800 m −0.024 s (45 of 70 faster) but 2,400–2,600 m +0.080 and 3,300–3,500 m +0.017 (the side switch in the flick and at the hairpin exit is delayed); 8 steps +0.123 (0.017); 4 steps without the bearing-jump condition +0.182 (0.023) with 1 of 40 off (bm14, `turn_grip_aero` +, 2,482 m). Above 120 km/h: −0.008 (0.016), 73.704; above 140 or 155 km/h (the same runs): −0.010 (0.014), 73.685, 2,700–2,800 m only −0.004; above 140 km/h and bends held ≥ 30 steps (this version): −0.007 (0.012), 73.681. The −0.024 s of the ungated form at 2,700–2,800 m came with the changed flick exit, not from the drops. |

Max \|trackPos\| 0.679 at ~2,476 m (flick; unchanged); slowest corner 55 km/h at ~3,273 m. **Suites on the committed (tagged) driver** (v1.01 in brackets): 73.649 / 73.738 / 73.657 (73.660 / 73.711 / 73.658), **all-30 73.681** (73.676), max 0.773 (`turn_grip_aero` +, flick left arc 2,462 m) / 0.789 (`brake_margin` −2, flick 2,475 m) / 0.749 (`line_aim_off` −0.2, 2,477 m), **0 of 30 off**. **Shifted bases 0 of 40 off**: bm14 73.751 (73.773), on-track max 0.904 (`brake_margin` −2, hairpin exit 3,279 m; was 0.893); lo05 73.753 (73.762), max 0.802 (`turn_grip_aero` +, flick left arc 2,462 m; was 0.810). **Paired per run against v1.01:** 30 suite runs **+0.005 s** (SE 0.009; 7 faster, 11 slower, 12 identical); 40 shifted runs −0.016 s (SE 0.020; 15 faster, 10 slower, 15 identical); **all 70 −0.007 s (SE 0.012; 22 faster, 21 slower, 27 identical)**. Section means over the 70 runs: 1,000–1,400 m −0.006 (one run), 2,600–2,900 m −0.008, 2,900–3,100 m +0.008, rest within ±0.002. The runs that still drop and re-detect at 2,700–2,800 m (11) are now only 0.009 s slower over 2,700–2,900 m than the others (was 0.072 s with 14), yet the section mean moved by −0.007 s: the old 0.072 s was mostly not caused by the drop. `tools/patterns.py` (v1.01 → v1.02, single lap): 41 → 44 reversals, 1 episode 0.3 s at 536–546 m (same), 102 → 107 target flips, 99 → 96 plan jumps, 34 brake applications of which 21 short and 14 re-applied within 10 steps (same), 47 shifts with 3 undone (same), TC 14.6 → 14.3 s. `tools/width.py`: unchanged within 0.01 (446 m +0.21 / +0.11 / +0.36; 770 m +0.21 / −0.15 / +0.30; 1,042 m +0.37 / −0.46 / +0.19; 1,528 m +0.09 / −0.41 / −0.02; 1,927 m +0.25 / −0.14 / +0.10; 2,674 m −0.17 / −0.26 / −0.35; 2,988 m +0.13 / −0.46 / +0.03). |
| **Decision** | ❌ Rejected — 0 damage, 0 of 30 off and shifted bases 0 of 40 off, but all-30 73.681 is above v1.01's 73.676 and the paired gain over all 70 runs is −0.007 s at 0.6 standard errors (22 faster, 21 slower), with no pattern benefit and the bm14 shifted max up from 0.893 to 0.904: inside the noise. Driver restored to v1.01 (`git checkout v1.01 -- snakeoil3_v1.py`); the best single lap of a kept version stays 1:13.59. |
| **Learned** | (1) **The remaining tails have no single removable cause except the start kink, and the kink's dab is at its optimum**: `dab_steer` 0.20–0.30 is +0.001 to +0.004 s and `dab_v` 220 is +0.014 s over 70 runs; restoring the throttle after a short brake is +0.000 to +0.003 s; what the 14 kink-slow runs still lose (0.018 s over all 70) is the brake itself, a four-step false plan dip. (2) **The turn-in step is needed even flat-out**: a softer steering step above 200 km/h at full throttle is 0.066–0.177 s slower over 70 runs and takes the shifted max to 0.975. (3) **The −12° beam grazing the inside edge mid-bend is a real, clean event** (14 of 70 runs at 2,714–2,751 m: 62–69 m → 17–18 m for 2–3 steps, bearing to ~0°, bend dropped and on 6 of them not detected again), **but removing it is worth nothing measurable**: −0.007 s (SE 0.012) over 70 runs with the debounce gated to above 140 km/h and bends held ≥ 30 steps; −0.010 s (SE 0.014) with bends held ≥ 10 steps. The 0.072 s by which the runs with the event were slower was a correlation, not its cost. With v0.98 this closes mid-bend drops in both forms (bearing touching 1.00° and beam collapse). (4) **A debounce on bend drops must stay away from the flick and the hairpin exit**: ungated it costs 0.080 s at 2,400–2,600 m and 0.017 s at 3,300–3,500 m, and without the bearing-jump condition one shifted run leaves the track at the flick. (5) **Comparing runs with and without an event overstates its cost**; the estimate has to be confirmed by removing the event (the start kink passed that test in v1.01, the beam graze did not). (6) The finish-line false braking starts on the last one or two logged steps before the line on 53 of 70 runs: under 0.001 s, closed. (7) `lock_gear_v` is dead between 105 and 120 (first gear is always taken at 82–96 km/h). (8) Races used: about 1,471 in 17 minutes of exploring. |

## v1.03 — Soft dab: a brake application from flat-out in a held bend starts at a tenth of the pedal (new `soft_n` 2 / `soft_brake` 0.1 / `soft_thr` 0.5 / `soft_steer` 0.25)

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v1.03 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v1.03`; **rejected, the driver at HEAD is restored to v1.01**). One mechanism: **soft dab.** A brake application that starts in a held bend (a bend side is set) from at least `soft_thr` 0.5 of stored throttle sends only `soft_brake` 0.1 of its pedal for its first `soft_n` 2 steps, while the smoothed \|steer\| is below `soft_steer` 0.25 and the speed is below `dab_v` 230 km/h; from the third step the full pedal applies. Aimed at the start kink's second false plan dip (183–190 m). New knobs at lines 619–622 (`soft_n` 0 = off, reproduces v1.01: suite 1 73.660, max 0.761); code at lines 959–964. In the tagged driver v1.01's lines 619–954 moved +4, line 955 +8 and lines ≥ 956 +9. No other value changed. Also in this iteration, as its own commit (`878f596`, no tag): `tools/tails.py`, the tail analysis and paired comparison over the 70 standard runs. |
| **Why** | Own analysis first, with the new `tools/tails.py` on v1.01's 70 runs re-run with kept telemetry (73.660 / 73.711 / 73.658, all-30 73.676, bm14 73.773, lo05 73.762: identical to the reference; mean lap 73.728 s, sum of per-section minima 72.536). **(1) Lead 1, the 2,900–3,100 m tail, traced step by step from 2,850 m** (normal run: unperturbed; tail runs: `line_aim_off` +0.2, lo05 with `brake_margin` +2, bm14 with `tc_slip_straight` +1, bm14 with `brake_aero` +). The bulk of the 70 runs take 4.00–4.04 s over the two sections with a slowest point of 140–144 km/h at 2,975–2,990 m. The tail is two different things. **(a) Three runs (4.20 / 4.24 / 4.38 s, slowest point 130.2 / 129.5 / 123.7 km/h) drop the bend mid-corner.** Through the whole bend the look-ahead bearing sits just above the drop threshold `line_aim_off` 1.0° (unperturbed run: 1.45° at 2,945 m, 1.17° at 2,957–2,961 m, **minimum 1.03° at 2,970–2,972 m**). On two of the three it touches the threshold (1.19° at 2,956 m with `line_aim_off` 1.2; 1.00° at 2,972 m on the other): the bend is dropped, the line target goes, the steering unwinds from −0.16 / −0.24 to +0.24 / +0.09 within four steps, the nose swings to the outside (angle to the track 7.0° → 0.2° / 5.3° → 0.3°), the bend is detected again with the bearing at 3.7–3.8° and 30–31 m of road in view instead of 35–36 m, the plan falls 149 → 131 km/h (142 → 129), the car brakes at pedal 0.2–0.63 for about 8–10 steps and takes 2nd gear at 131 km/h (the normal run stays in 3rd and does not brake after 2,950 m). On the third (bm14, `tc_slip_straight` +1) the bearing collapses 1.57° → 0.25° in one step at 2,938 m (a beam closing, as at 2,714–2,751 m in v1.02), the steering goes to +0.60, the plan to 120 km/h, full brake for about six steps, 3 → 2 at 160 km/h, slowest point 123.7 km/h. **(b) About ten runs (4.04–4.16 s, slowest point 136.9–140 km/h at 2,988–2,999 m) are perturbations that brake later or run a wider line** (`brake_aero` +, `brake_decel` +0.5, `brake_margin` −2, `corner_speed` +2, `line_offset` +0.1, mostly on the bm14 base). Traced on bm14 with `brake_aero` +: it rides a plan about 4 km/h higher at 2,956 m (155.5 against 151.9 km/h), then the road in view shrinks 35 → 33 m, the plan falls 150 → 137 km/h and the car brakes for about six steps (pedal 0.27–0.42, 150 → 138 km/h) and restarts the throttle from its floor. That is the perturbation itself, not a chance event. **Confirmed by removing the event** (position-gated diagnostic only, never a version: at 2,930–3,000 m the bend is not dropped above 0.5° of bearing while the car steers more than 0.1 into it): **2 of 70 runs change, both faster; all 70 −0.006 s (SE 0.004)**, all-30 73.669, 2,900–3,000 m −0.002 and 3,000–3,100 m −0.003 s. So of the table's 0.019 s for this tail about 0.006 s is removable in principle, on two runs. **Inherited conclusion challenged:** lead 1 treats the tail as one untraced cause worth 0.019 s; it is mostly the suite's braking perturbations arriving hot, plus the mid-bend drop that v0.98 and v1.02 already measured. **Candidates, all paired against the re-run v1.01 over the 70 runs** (mean, SE; all-30; on-track max suites / shifted): (A) own: **bend not dropped above 0.5° while the car steers > 0.1 into it, without the position gate**: +0.255 s (0.019), 73.923, 1 of 40 off (bm14, `turn_grip_aero` +, 2,478 m), max 0.937 / 0.952; lost at 700–800 m +0.074, 1,000–1,100 m +0.040, 1,500–1,600 m +0.057; at 2,900–3,100 m +0.005. (B) `line_aim_off` 0.9 as the base value: +0.110 s (0.023), 73.782, shifted max 0.914; 1,000–1,100 m +0.083, 1,500–1,600 m +0.035. (C) own, new: **coasting lift** (in the lift band the engine's zero-torque throttle is sent instead of 0, so a lift is a coast and not engine braking; the unperturbed run alternates throttle 0.4–0.9 with 4–5-step lifts through 2,953–2,990 m): graded from the plan to the band edge +0.051 s (0.019), 73.756, shifted max 0.981; flat +0.074 s (0.020), 1 of 40 off (bm14, `turn_grip_aero` +, 2,482 m); half of it flat +0.032 s (0.018), 1 of 40 off; twice graded +0.071 s (0.022), 1 of 40 off: the engine braking in the lift band is needed, most at the flick (2,500–2,600 m +0.016 to +0.024). (D) lead 2, own, new: **soft dab** (this version's mechanism). With v1.01's dab gates only (smoothed \|steer\| < 0.15, < 230 km/h), 4 steps at half pedal: +0.057 s (0.020), 73.757; 200–400 m −0.012 s but 1,900–2,000 m +0.031 and 2,500–2,600 m +0.015. Only from ≥ 0.5 stored throttle: 4 steps at 0.5 → +0.024 s (0.018), 73.729, **shifted max 0.986** (bm14, `turn_grip` +0.5, flick left arc 2,462 m); at 0.25 → +0.037 s (0.020), 73.758, max 0.900; 6 steps at 0.5 → +0.043 s (0.018), 73.753, max 0.940. Also only at 212–230 km/h: 4 steps at 0.5 → +0.029 s (0.018), 73.742, max 0.986; at 0.1 → +0.028 s (0.021), 73.755, max 0.902; 8 steps at 0.5 → +0.073 s (0.018), 73.801; 2 steps at 0.1 → −0.003 s (0.018), 73.722, max 0.884 / 0.914. Only in a held bend with the smoothed \|steer\| < 0.25, from ≥ 0.5 throttle: 4 steps at 0.5 → +0.039 s (0.022), 73.761, max 0.891 / 0.882; **2 steps at 0.1 (this version) → −0.003 s (0.018)**, 73.723. Every form gains 0.005–0.012 s at 200–400 m and gives it back at 700–800 m and at the flick: the braking for the 770 m bend starts at 229 km/h, from full throttle, with a straight wheel and with the bend already held, so nothing the car senses tells it from the kink's false dip. A pedal share of 0 cannot be used in this form (the application counter resets on a zero pedal and the car never brakes: all 70 off). |

Section means over the 70 runs: 200–300 m −0.008 (runs more than 0.03 s above the median 14 → 8, tail loss 0.018 → 0.008 s), 2,700–2,900 m −0.012, 1,500–1,600 m +0.006, 2,400–2,600 m +0.014, the rest within ±0.004. `tools/patterns.py` (v1.01 → v1.03, single lap): 41 → 38 reversals, 1 episode 0.3 s at 536–546 m (same), 102 → 101 target flips, 99 → 95 plan jumps, 34 → 32 brake applications of which 21 → 20 short and 14 re-applied within 10 steps (same), 47 shifts with 3 undone (same), TC 14.6 → 14.3 s. `tools/width.py` (entry / slowest point / exit): 446 m unchanged (+0.21 / +0.11 / +0.36); 770 m +0.21 / −0.15 / +0.30 → 765 m +0.21 / −0.14 / +0.46; 1,042 m +0.37 / −0.46 / +0.19 → +0.41 / −0.48 / +0.18; 1,528 m +0.09 / −0.41 / −0.02 → 1,525 m +0.09 / −0.41 / −0.12; 1,927 m +0.25 / −0.14 / +0.10 → 1,928 m +0.30 / −0.20 / +0.07; 2,988 m +0.14 / −0.46 / +0.03 → 2,989 m +0.12 / −0.46 / +0.02: knock-ons of the changed 770 m braking, no intended line change. |
| **Decision** | ❌ Rejected — 0 damage, 0 of 30 off and shifted bases 0 of 40 off, but all-30 73.723 is above v1.01's 73.676 (paired +0.046 s at 2.0 standard errors), the paired difference over all 70 runs is −0.003 s at 0.2 standard errors (32 faster, 37 slower), the single lap is 0.036 s slower, and margin is spent at the flick's left arc (suite max 0.789 → 0.884, shifted max 0.893 → 0.914). Driver restored to v1.01 (`git checkout v1.01 -- snakeoil3_v1.py`); the best single lap of a kept version stays 1:13.59. |
| **Learned** | (1) **The 2,900–3,100 m tail is traced, and it is not one event.** Three of 70 runs drop the bend mid-corner (the bearing rides at 1.03–1.2° through 2,955–2,985 m against the 1.0° drop threshold; two touch it, one has a one-step beam collapse to 0.25° at 2,938 m), unwind the steering, re-detect with 30 m of road in view, brake to 124–130 km/h and take 2nd; about ten more runs are braking or line perturbations that arrive hot and brake for about six steps at 2,975–2,982 m, which is the perturbation and not chance. (2) **Removing the mid-bend drop there is worth −0.006 s (SE 0.004) over 70 runs, on 2 runs**, measured with a position-gated diagnostic; the same rule without the position gate is +0.255 s with 1 of 40 off, and `line_aim_off` 0.9 is +0.110 s. With v0.98 and v1.02 this closes the tail: the comparison figure of 0.019 s was again about three times what removal gives. (3) **A softer start of a brake application reaches the kink but cannot be separated from real braking**: every form gains 0.005–0.012 s at 200–400 m (kink-slow runs 14 → 8 of 70 in this version) and loses it at 700–800 m and at the flick, because the 770 m braking starts at 229 km/h from full throttle with a straight wheel and the bend already held; gates on stored throttle, a 212–230 km/h window and a held bend all leave nearly every run changed (69 of 70 in this version). Best form −0.003 s (SE 0.018) with all-30 +0.046 s and the flick margin spent. Brake-side fixes for the kink are closed like the throttle-side and plan-side ones. (4) **The engine braking in the lift band is needed**: sending the zero-torque throttle during a lift is 0.03–0.07 s slower over 70 runs and puts one shifted run off at the flick in three of four forms. (5) `tools/tails.py` reproduces v1.01's reference numbers and gives the tail table and the paired comparison in one call of about 40 s per config. (6) View: nothing found here is above the 70-run noise without knowing which corner is coming; the kink (0.018 s), this bend's drop (0.006 s) and the flick arrival all need track memory. (7) Races used: 1,481 in 16 minutes of exploring. |

## v1.04 — S-bend look with the focus sensors: a slow corner behind a kink caps the plan (new `sb_a` 12 / `sb_v` 200 / `sb_steer` 0.3 / `sb_fall` 3 / `sb_x` 25 / `sb_hold` 12)

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v1.04 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v1.04`). One mechanism: **S-bend look.** The driver now uses the server's `focus` sensors for the first time. **Request** (lines 1163–1171, end of `drive_example()`): when the longest track beam is at least `sb_a` 12° off the nose, its outer neighbour is under half of it (the inside edge of a kink), the speed is above `sb_v` 200 km/h and the smoothed \|steer\| is below `sb_steer` 0.3, the client asks for a focus reading centred 2° on the nose side of that beam (`R['focus']= [±10]` or `[±17]`: five rays 1° apart covering the 4° inside the beam); otherwise it sends `[100]`, which the server treats as "no request". **Use** (lines 898–924, after the sharpness plan): if in the reading every ray is longer than half the longest and the ray nearest the nose is more than `sb_fall` 3 m longer than the one at the beam's angle (the far edge comes closer as the angle grows: the road turns back behind the kink), the allowed speed is capped for `sb_hold` 12 steps at the braking distance to `corner_speed` over (longest ray − distance driven since − `sb_x` 25 m). New knobs at lines 630–635 (`sb_hold` 0 = looks only: all 70 standard runs identical to v1.01). Telemetry: six columns appended after `track18`: `focA` (angle asked for, 100 = none) and `foc0`–`foc4` (−1 = no reading); `lap_report.py`, `tools/metrics.py`, `tools/width.py`, `tools/patterns.py` and `tools/tails.py` read the new CSV unchanged. Line mapping from v1.01: old 630–891 +6, old 892–1129 +33, old ≥ 1130 +42. No other value changed. |
| **Why** | Assignment: give the car more to see, within one lap and without memory, starting with the `focus` sensors. **(1) Facts about focus in this build** (`scr_server.cpp` lines 312–314, 405–438, 624; `CarControl.cpp` lines 79–82; `sensors.cpp`; then measured). The command is **one integer** (the server parses the first number after `focus`): the centre angle in degrees from the nose, valid −90 … +90; the five rays sit at centre −2 … +2, 1° apart, range 200 m, same 2D edge geometry as the track beams (all −1 off the track), no noise in this build. A reading is sent only when the cooldown is over, then the cooldown is set to 1 s: measured one reading every 46–50 client steps (1.01 s of lap time), the other steps read −1. A command outside ±90 is turned into "no request" (360): it returns −1 and **does not start the cooldown**, so the client can hold its one reading per second until it wants it. The reading uses the angle sent on the previous step. Until now the client sent `(focus -90 -45 0 45 90)`, which the server read as −90: a reading at −92 … −88° each second that nobody looked at. **(2) What the focus rays show** (diagnostic variant, 157 laps, driving identical: centres −28 … +28 in steps of 5 at eight phases = a 1° scan of ±30° at 589 positions of the v1.01 lap). **Start kink:** the longest sightline is always in the gap between the −7° and −12° beams: at 158.9 m (222.7 km/h, allowed 223.5: the first false dip) −7° 80 m, −12° 37 m, but −8 / −9 / −10 / −11°: 84 / 90 / 96 / 103 m; at 182.3 m (the second dip, allowed 235) −7° 88 m, −12° 28 m, −9 / −10 / −11°: 111 / 127 / 149 m; at 189 m −11° already 200 m. **Flick approach:** no ray sees further than the track beams' longest by more than 17 m (2,275–2,400 m: +0 to +9 m, once +17 m at 2,312 m); the longest ray reaches the flick's arc (~2,460 m) from 2,334 m, so the flick is not hidden, it is not recognised. But the rays show its shape: at 2,346 m +4 / +5 / +6 / +7°: 61 / 67 / 74 / 84 m, then a jump at +8°: 112 m, falling to 110 / 108 / 106 / 103 / 100 / 96 / 89 m at +15°, then the kink's inside edge (+16°: 24 m). At the start kink the rays rise all the way to the inside edge. The track beams (+4° 61, +7° 84, +12° 103, +19° 16 m) look the same in both. **Inherited conclusion challenged:** "no signal before 2,365 m separates … the beams show the slow corner only from 2,376 m" (v0.99, rank 4) holds for the 19 beams only. **Candidates, all paired against the re-run v1.01 over the 70 runs** (mean, SE; all-30; on-track max suites / shifted; base re-run 73.660 / 73.711 / 73.658, all-30 73.676, bm14 73.773, lo05 73.762: identical to the reference). **(A) own: gap look for the start kink** (when the car is over the plan with a straight wheel at 200–240 km/h, look into the gap beside the plan's binding beam; the look's rays join the sharpness plan as extra beams, the best one held for 30 steps and counted down by the distance driven): every look counted: +0.141 s (0.023), 73.839, 1 of 40 off (bm14, `brake_margin` −2, hairpin exit 3,293 m), 100–400 m −0.018 s but 700–800 m +0.063, 1,500–1,600 m +0.045, 3,200–3,300 m +0.023: at real braking zones the gap ray is 2–5 m longer than the binding beam and the car brakes later; requested after 2 steps over the plan: +0.141 s (0.022), 1 of 40 off; up to 300 km/h: +0.077 s (0.023), 73.786, max 0.874 / 0.932; counted only when ≥ 12 m longer than the binding beam (kink +22 to +59 m, braking zones +2 to +5 m): +0.017 s (0.018), 73.747, 1 of 40 off (bm14, `turn_grip_aero` +, flick 2,483 m), 100–300 m −0.015 s; ≥ 20 m: +0.006 s (0.017), 73.734, 1 of 40 off (same run); ≥ 12 m and requested after 2 steps over the plan: +0.003 s (0.015), 73.713, 0 off, max 0.896 / 0.899; ≥ 12 m up to 300 km/h: +0.011 s (0.018), 1 of 40 off. In the last 2-step form 35 of 70 runs change at the kink: −0.025 s at 400 m (SE 0.011), −0.007 s (SE 0.020) by 2,300 m, +0.019 s (SE 0.028) at the line: the kink's time is recovered and the flick then scatters it, as in v0.99, v1.01's neighbours and v1.03. **(B) own, new: S-bend look** (this version). Looks only (`sb_hold` 0): on all 70 runs one look lands at 2,335–2,338 m (242–252 km/h) and **all 70 show the falling far edge** (124 / 122 / 120 / 117 / 114 m, fall 9 m); of the 534 looks elsewhere (150–200 m: 70, rays rising by 25–27 m; 600–650 m: 70; 700–750 m: 66; 950–1,000 m: 70; 1,450–1,500 m: 70; 2,000–2,050 m: 69; 2,600–2,650 m: 45; 2,900–2,950 m: 69; 350–400 m: 5) **none does**. Cap forms (`sb_x` / `sb_hold`): 22 / 12 → +0.024 s (0.014), 73.699, max 0.793 / 0.920; **25 / 12 (this version) → −0.035 s (0.014)**, 73.661, max 0.803 / 0.896; 28 / 12 → −0.028 s (0.016), 73.668, max 0.806 / 0.923 (bm14, `brake_margin` −2, flick 2,474 m); 32 / 12 → −0.017 s (0.014), 73.671, max 0.798 / 0.890; 22 / 25 → −0.026 s (0.014), 73.675, max 0.905 / 0.986 (bm14, `brake_margin` −2, flick 2,474 m). Not trialled for lack of races: sources (2) beam layout and (3) edge geometry from neighbouring beams; the look's rays used for (3) would be the natural form. |

| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — 0 damage, 0 of 30 off, shifted bases 0 of 40 off, all-30 73.661 (v1.01 73.676), paired gain over all 70 runs −0.035 s at 2.5 standard errors (43 faster, 26 slower, 1 identical), and the margin is not spent: suite max 0.789 → 0.803 and shifted max 0.893 → 0.896 (hairpin exit), while the worst flick run of the 70 goes 0.863 → 0.840. The single lap is 0.020 s slower than v1.01's (1:13.61 against 1:13.59; one lap, lost at the flick's exit), so the best single lap stays 1:13.59. To be weighed by the orchestrator: `sb_x` 25 is the best of five settings tried (the others: +0.024, −0.028, −0.017, −0.026 s), so the true gain is more likely 0.02–0.03 s than 0.035, and its neighbour 22 does not gain. |
| **Learned** | (1) **The focus sensors work in this build and add information the 19 beams do not have**: five rays 1° apart at any centre angle within ±90° (one integer command), one reading per 1.01 s (46–50 steps), and none is used up while the client sends an out-of-range angle, so a look can be taken exactly when it is wanted. They see no further than a straight ray can (the flick's arc is reached by the longest ray from 2,334 m, +0 to +17 m over the track beams on that approach), but they show the shape of what is seen. (2) **A look separates the flick approach from every other kink on all 70 runs**: one look at 2,335–2,338 m shows the far edge coming closer with the angle (124 → 114 m over 4°) on 70 of 70 runs; none of 534 looks at the other places does (start kink: rays rising by 25–27 m toward the inside edge). That is 38–41 m, about 0.6 s, before the 2,376 m where the track beams show the flick, and before the chance plan dip at 2,345–2,349 m that used to set the arrival speed. Caveats: the rule is tested on this track only, and at 2,600–2,650 m some looks fall by up to 37 m and are rejected only by the "every ray longer than half the longest" condition. (3) **Capping the plan from that look makes the flick arrival the same on every run and gains time**: speed at 2,365 m 228–246 → 230–239 km/h, 2,300–2,600 m standard deviation 0.057 → 0.037 s, all 70 −0.035 s (SE 0.014); the gain is at 2,400–2,500 m (−0.024 s), part given back at 2,500–2,600 m (+0.013 s). (4) **Slowing earlier before the kink makes the car faster at the flick's arc entry, not slower**: with `sb_x` 22 / `sb_hold` 25 the 70 runs pass 2,365 m at 217–228 km/h but 2,445 m at 89.5–96.4 km/h (v1.01 82.9–94.2) and run to 0.986 at 2,474 m; with 28 / 12: 0.923. The car that arrives slower is less far over the plan in the kink, brakes with less lock and carries more speed into the arc. So the cap must stay short and shallow; `sb_x` 22 is too shallow (it only shifts the dip, 236–243 km/h at 2,365 m, +0.024 s). (5) **The start kink's false dip can be removed with a gap look** (the true sightline is at −8 … −11°, 20–60 m longer than the −7° beam; 100–300 m −0.011 to −0.015 s over 70 runs), **but not paid out**: the gain is under the scatter the flick adds afterwards (best form +0.003 s, SE 0.015), and any form that counts the 2–5 m a gap ray adds at real braking zones is 0.08–0.14 s slower with one run off. Now that the flick arrival no longer depends on a chance dip, this is worth one re-test on v1.04. (6) Not measured here, for the next agent: on v1.04 the changes the flick approach used to block (`tc_slip_straight` 8, `brake_hi_v` 245 / `brake_max_hi` ungated, `dab_v` 250); a look-keyed lower end speed instead of the 25 m; `sb_hold` 8 / 16 and `sb_fall` neighbours; the look's rays as extra plan beams in the medium bends (per-bend plan headroom). (7) Races used: 1,491 in about 22 minutes of exploring; no waiting for the user's TORCS. |

| **Version** | v1.05 |

One mechanism: **the planned-deceleration cap's gate is lifted at speed.** Since v0.95 the braking plan may assume `brake_max_hi` 34 m/s² instead of `brake_max` 30 only where that lifts the allowed speed no higher than `brake_hi_v` 235 km/h; the gate existed for the flick approach alone. Flick over the 70 runs: look at 2,335–2,338 m on 70 of 70; speed at 2,365 m 230.0–238.6 → 230.7–239.6 km/h; at 2,445 m 82.8–93.2 → 83.8–94.4 km/h; time over 2,300–2,600 m mean 8.440 → 8.434 s, standard deviation 0.037 → 0.039, slowest 8.539 → 8.570. `tools/patterns.py` (v1.04 → v1.05, single lap): 38 → 42 reversals, 1 episode 0.3 s at 536–546 m → 0 episodes, 103 → 102 target flips, 94 → 95 plan jumps, 36 → 33 brake applications of which 22 → 21 short and 15 → 14 re-applied within 10 steps, 45 → 47 shifts with 3 → 4 undone within 1 s (new: 3-4-3 at 2,603 m, after the flick exit; the other three are the first-gear-at-full-lock stints at 2,472, 2,487 and 3,256 m as before), TC 14.5 → 14.2 s. `tools/width.py` (entry / slowest point / exit): 446 m +0.21 / +0.11 / +0.36 → 447 m +0.21 / +0.11 / +0.35; 770 m +0.21 / −0.15 / +0.30 → 769 m +0.21 / −0.16 / +0.30; 1,042 m +0.37 / −0.46 / +0.19 → +0.37 / −0.45 / +0.19; 1,528 m +0.09 / −0.41 / −0.02 → +0.09 / −0.42 / −0.09; 1,927 m +0.25 / −0.14 / +0.10 (entry 255 km/h) → 1,931 m +0.31 / −0.22 / +0.02 (entry 261 km/h); flick 2,473 m −0.21 / +0.65 / −0.08 at 65 km/h → −0.21 / +0.66 / −0.07 at 66 km/h; 2,987 m +0.13 / −0.44 / +0.03 → 2,988 m +0.14 / −0.47 / +0.02; hairpin −0.22 / −0.49 / −0.41 → −0.23 / −0.48 / −0.41 at 54 km/h. |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — 0 damage, 0 of 30 off, shifted bases 0 of 40 off, all-30 73.642 (v1.04 73.661), paired gain over all 70 runs −0.033 s at 2.2 standard errors (42 faster, 25 slower, 3 identical), best single lap of a kept version (1:13.56). Margin: suite max 0.803 → 0.798, worst flick run of the 70 0.840 → 0.821, shifted max 0.896 → 0.904 (hairpin exit, `brake_margin` 14 with a further −2; +0.008, below the ~0.92 line). To be weighed by the orchestrator: 245 is one of three gate values tried (240: −0.030 s, 250: −0.033 s with all-30 73.685), chosen among about 20 configurations in all; what supports it beyond the lap difference is that the ungated cap and all three gate values show the same −0.015 to −0.019 s in each of the same three sections (about −0.048 s together), of which the chaotic rest of the lap leaves −0.030 to −0.033 s. |
| **Learned** | (1) **The flick approach no longer blocks the higher deceleration cap**: with `brake_max_hi` 34 everywhere all 70 runs stay on the track (suite max 0.789, shifted max 0.895; v0.95: 1 of 30 off, `brake_hi_v` 245: 2 of 30 off), because since v1.04 the look at 2,335–2,338 m sets the arrival speed and not the plan's early dips. The cap is worth −0.016 / −0.016 / −0.016 s in the braking zones at 1,400–1,500, 1,900–2,000 and 2,300–2,400 m (the car brakes about 4 m later at 266–283 km/h), measured the same in four configurations. (2) **What blocked it on v1.04 was the start kink**: ungated, 32 of 70 runs take an extra one-step brake at 180–181 m (+0.023 s at 200–300 m), so the lap gains only −0.005 s (SE 0.018). The kink is driven at 219–236 km/h and the three braking zones start at 266–283, so a gate on the car's own speed (240 / 245 / 250: −0.030 / −0.033 / −0.033 s over 70 runs) keeps the kink as it was. This is a speed separation, not knowledge of the kink; the kink's tail (14 of 70 runs, 0.018 s) is untouched. (3) `brake_hi_v` 245 (+0.024 s) and `brake_max_hi` 38 (−0.027 s with the shifted max at 0.919 on the flick's left arc) are not improvements; 34 and 235 stay. (4) **The other blocked changes are no longer cliffs but are not gains either**: `dab_v` 250 +0.024 s (SE 0.010), `lift_pct` 4.0 / 4.5 +0.020 / +0.051 s, all with 0 of 70 off. (5) **`tc_slip_straight` 8 is −0.025 s (SE 0.015) with no margin spent** (max 0.800 / 0.902) and stacks with this version (−0.049 s, SE 0.019, all-30 73.622, max 0.783 / 0.899); its gain is at the flick exit (2,500–2,800 m) and its cost at 400–600 m and 1,000–1,100 m in every value tried (6.5 / 7 / 8 / 10: +0.020 / −0.015 / −0.025 / −0.010 s), so the neighbours do not support 8 on its own: the next version's first candidate, to be judged on the stack against this version. (6) **The look's cap cannot be made longer or deeper**: `sb_hold` 16 slows the car at 2,365 m (221–230 km/h) and it then arrives faster at 2,445 m (88–95 km/h), −0.007 s with the lo05 flick max at 0.904; a sustained plan to the far edge is +0.035 to +0.122 s. `sb_fall` 5 changes nothing. (7) **The flick is still the largest tail** (2,500–2,600 m: 26 runs, 0.020 s on this version) and it is two groups: runs that finish the right arc by 2,500 m (2.91–2.93 s) and runs still at full lock in first gear there (3.05–3.07 s); the time through 2,400–2,600 m goes with the speed at 2,420 m (correlation +0.71: 137–141 km/h fast, 146–147 slow). Not turned into a mechanism. (8) Not measured: the gap look for the start kink (f), `brake_hi_v` 255, `brake_max_hi` 36, `tc_slip` 5.2–5.6, `dab_v` ungated, `sb_hold` 8, `sb_fall` 2, `sb_a`, `sb_v`, `sb_steer`. (9) Races used: 1,400 in 14 minutes of exploring, plus the final 70 and the version's run and rerun; no waiting for the user's TORCS. |

## v1.06 — MAJOR DESIGN CHANGE: track planning added to the rule-based driver. Corner table: per-bend offsets on the braking plan's allowed speed, keyed by `distFromStart` (new `corner_table`, 7 rows)

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v1.06 |
| **What changed** | **Major design change: from this version the driver plans with knowledge of the track.** Every earlier version (v0.1 to v1.05) was purely rule-based: it drove from the live sensor readings alone and knew nothing about the track beyond what its sensors showed at that moment. On 2026-10-04 the competition officials confirmed that a pre-mapped track may be used to anticipate turns and decide when to brake or accelerate, provided the sensors remain the main input ("track map = predicts what is coming; sensors = react to what is happening now"). v1.06 is the first version to use that: the rule-based sensor driver is unchanged and still makes every decision, and a hand-written table of corner positions tells it which corner is coming. Lap times from v1.06 on are therefore not like-for-like with those before it. Detail: `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v1.06`). One mechanism, **the first use of track memory** (allowed by the officials, 2026-10-04; hand-written from our telemetry, nothing learned on track, no stored steering or pedal traces): a **corner table** of rows (from m, to m, km/h). While `distFromStart` is inside a row, its offset is added to the allowed speed that the sensor-driven plan has just computed (road-ahead plan, sharpness plan, S-bend look); everything downstream (lift band, brake pedal, ABS, throttle, steering, line) is unchanged and still reacts to the live readings. New `corner_table` at lines 637–650 (comment 637–641, rows 643–649), applied at lines 941–949 (two code lines, 948–949, just before `c.allowed_speed=`). **Table as committed:** (950, 1065, +10) 1,042 m right-hander; (1395, 1585, +10) 1,528 m left-hander; (1835, 1945, +17) 1,931 m left-hander; (2585, 2648, +12) 2,600 m, downhill out of the Corkscrew; (2648, 2760, +17) 2,700 m left-hander; (2880, 3005, +17) 2,988 m right-hander; (3175, 3275, −3) hairpin. An empty table drives as v1.05. Line mapping from v1.05: old 637–926 +14, old ≥ 927 +23. No knob value changed. |
| **Why** | Assignment: per-bend plan headroom from a corner table (orchestrator's hypothesis from v1.00's position-gated diagnostics on v0.96), re-measured on v1.05. Base re-run with kept telemetry: 73.631 / 73.654 / 73.641, all-30 73.642, bm14 73.688, lo05 73.662 (identical to the reference); a zero table reproduces all 70 runs byte for byte. **Own analysis first** (v1.05 lap, step trace): in every medium bend the car rides the plan (speed within 1–3 km/h of the allowed speed from the end of braking to the slowest point: 1,000–1,049 m, 1,482–1,531 m, 1,912–1,936 m, 2,664–2,739 m, 2,938–2,988 m) with the steering at 0.25–0.47 and, over the 70 runs, never more than 0.27–0.55 of the half-width used there; at 2,602–2,640 m (downhill out of the Corkscrew, 192–200 km/h) it lifts on the plan with no bend in the width table at all. So the plan, not the tyres or the edge, sets those speeds, and the table can say where. **Own candidates:** (1) the table as one offset per bend over braking zone and bend; (2) new: a row for 2,585–2,648 m, a plan-limited stretch nobody had listed as a corner; (3) new: split rows, a different offset for the braking zone and for the bend itself; (4) new: a negative row at the hairpin to buy margin where the shifted maximum sits; (5) a row lifting the start kink's "false" plan dip; (6) a row slowing the flick approach (the arrival speed at 2,365 m). All are rows of the same table, measured over all 70 paired runs against the re-run v1.05 (zones as in the table unless noted; 0 off unless noted). **Screening, combined rows read per section:** +4 at 446 / 770 / 1,042 / 1,528 / 1,931 / 2,700 / 2,988 m: −0.104 s (SE 0.020; 53 / 17 / 0), all-30 73.544, max 0.777 / 0.899; −4 at the same seven: +0.391 s (SE 0.030; 2 / 68 / 0). +8 at 1,528 / 1,931 / 2,700 / 2,988 m: −0.214 s (SE 0.014; 68 / 2 / 0), 73.419, max 0.766 / 0.901. +14 at those four with +10 at 2,600 m and +4 at 1,042 m: −0.389 s (SE 0.021; 69 / 1 / 0), 73.212, 0.783 / 0.897. +20 at 1,931 / 2,700 / 2,988 m (+8 at 1,042 and 1,528, +10 at 2,600, hairpin −3): −0.370 s (SE 0.019), 73.262, 0.806 / 0.873, worst \|trackPos\| at 1,900–2,050 m 0.727. **+28 at those three: 4 of 70 off around 1,960 m**, +0.284 s. **Per bend (section sums over the zone and its exit):** 1,042 m +4 / +8 / +10: −0.014 to −0.024 / −0.045 / −0.042 s; 1,528 m +4 / +8 / +10 / +14: −0.021 / −0.023 / −0.023 / −0.017 s (flat: 1,400–1,500 m −0.031 to −0.097 s, given back at 1,500–1,700 m); 1,931 m +8 / +14 / +17 / +20: −0.071 / −0.096 to −0.127 / −0.105 / −0.102 s; 2,600 m +10 alone: −0.045 to −0.055 s (+20: the same); 2,700 m +4 / +8: −0.036 / −0.054 s; 2,600 + 2,700 m together +10 / +14, +12 / +17, +10 / +20: −0.104 / −0.114 / −0.092 s; 2,988 m +8 / +14 / +17 / +20: −0.064 / −0.130 / −0.134 / −0.127 s. **Bends left at 0, every offset slower:** 446 m: +4 +0.017 s (300–400 m −0.025, 400–600 m +0.042), −4 +0.010, braking zone +4 with the bend −4 −0.003 (400–500 m +0.070, 500–600 m −0.043), braking zone +8 +0.049; 770 m: +4 +0.008, −4 +0.019, braking zone +4 with the bend −3 +0.053. **Split rows (3) lose:** braking zone only +8 at the four bends: 1,931 m +0.132 s, 1,528 m +0.036 s (the car brakes later and then has to brake in the bend); bend only +8: 1,528 m +0.010, 1,931 m −0.079, 2,988 m −0.005 (each less than the whole row's gain); 1,042 m braking zone only +8: +0.012. **Start kink (5), 150–215 m:** +15: 200–300 m +0.023 s; +40: +0.038 s there and all 70 +0.075 s (SE 0.017; The plan dip there is a local optimum against a table offset in both directions. **Flick approach (6), 2,325–2,385 m −5:** 2,300–2,400 m +0.021, 2,500–2,600 m −0.050 s, all 70 −0.020 s with the hairpin row (SE 0.012), but the flick margin goes (suite max 0.886, shifted 0.966 at 2,474–2,476 m): not taken. **Hairpin (4), 3,175–3,275 m:** +3: 3,200–3,300 m +0.025 s, exit at 0.948–0.960 of the edge and 1 of 70 off; **−3: −0.009 s (−0.404 against −0.395 s with the same other rows) and the hairpin exit's worst run 0.904 → 0.747–0.770.** **Composed tables:** 8 / 8 / 14 / 10 / 14 / 14 without the hairpin row: −0.395 s (SE 0.017; 70 / 0 / 0), 73.231, 0.782 / 0.902; with it: −0.404 s (SE 0.016; 70 / 0 / 0), 73.228, 0.782 / 0.875; **10 / 10 / 17 / 12 / 17 / 17 / −3 (this version): −0.427 s (SE 0.016; 70 / 0 / 0)**, 73.191, 0.784 / 0.818. **Cliffs:** every offset ×0.8: −0.355 s (SE 0.014), 73.269, 0.785 / 0.833; ×1.2 of the 8 / 14 table (9.6 / 16.8 / 12): −0.444 s (SE 0.017), 73.169, 0.798 / 0.810; **every row 20 m earlier: +0.363 s** (SE 0.022; 2 / 68 / 0; the offset ends before the slowest point and the car brakes mid-bend: 1,900–2,000 m +0.159, 2,700–2,900 m +0.145, 3,000–3,100 m +0.140), 0 off, max 0.783 / 0.804; every row 20 m later: −0.236 s (SE 0.020), 0 off, max 0.781 / 0.822; **every row starting 30 m earlier, with the offset doubled over the 30 m before the bend (a trial whose split rows overlapped: +34 km/h at 1,885–1,915 m): 14 of 70 off, the worst at 1,956–1,958 m**; every row ending 15 m later, with the offset doubled over 15 m inside the bend (same error): −0.139 s (SE 0.020), 0 off, max 0.800 / 0.887. Not measured: rows starting earlier or ending later on their own (the two trials meant for it overlapped the rows instead), offsets above +10 at 1,042 m and 1,528 m alone, a ramp at the row ends, a grip factor instead of a km/h offset, `tc_slip_straight` 8 on this car. |

Max \|trackPos\| 0.677 at ~2,477 m (flick; was 0.672); slowest corner 58 km/h at ~3,273 m (hairpin; was 54). | **Decision** | ✅ Kept — 0 damage, 0 of 30 off, shifted bases 0 of 40 off, all-30 73.191 (v1.05 73.642), paired gain over all 70 runs −0.427 s at 26 standard errors with 70 of 70 runs faster, the gain inside the table's zones (−0.418 s of it), best single lap 1:13.14. Margin: suite max 0.798 → 0.784, shifted max 0.904 → 0.818 (the hairpin row), so margin was gained, not spent. The one place where room was used is 1,931 m (worst run 0.27 → 0.66 of the half-width); its cliff is at +28 km/h (off around 1,960 m), +20 is still on track at 0.727. |
| **Learned** | (1) **In five medium bends and on the downhill out of the Corkscrew the plan was the limit, not the car**: +10 to +17 km/h of allowed speed is worth 0.43 s over 70 perturbed runs with no margin spent. The sharpness plan's one grip figure fits 446 m and 770 m (every offset there is slower, in both directions) and undersells 1,042, 1,528, 1,931, 2,700 and 2,988 m. v1.00's signs hold for 1,528 and 1,931 m and are reversed for 1,042 and 2,988 m (+5 was +0.041 / +0.032 s on v0.96's suite 1; now −0.042 / −0.134 s at +10 / +17 over 70 runs). (2) **The offset must cover the braking zone and the bend as one row.** Braking zone only is slower at 1,931 m by 0.13 s (the car arrives too fast and brakes in the bend); a row that ends 20 m too early is 0.36 s slower over the lap; a double offset over the 30 m before the 1,931 m bend leaves the track on 14 of 70 runs. Row ends are the sensitive part: keep them past the slowest point, where the plan no longer binds. (3) **The optimum is about +14 to +17 at 1,931, 2,700 and 2,988 m**; +20 is no faster and +28 is off at 1,931 m. 1,528 m is flat from +4 to +14 (what later braking gains at 1,400–1,500 m is given back at 1,500–1,700 m: the bend's exit, not its entry, is the open question there). (4) **A plan-limited stretch that is not a corner**: 2,585–2,648 m, where the car lifted on the plan at 192–200 km/h; +10 to +12 is 0.05 s, +20 no more. (5) **The hairpin wants a slower entry**: −3 km/h costs no time (−0.009 s) and takes the hairpin exit's worst run from 0.904 to 0.770; +3 goes to 0.95–0.96 with 1 of 70 off. The shifted maximum is now the flick (0.818). Larger negative values not measured. (6) **The start kink's plan dip is not simply false**: lifting it with the table (+15 / +40 km/h over 150–215 m) is 0.023 / 0.038 s slower in 200–300 m and +0.075 s on the lap, and −6 is slower too. Its 14-run tail (0.018 s) is unchanged. A table row cannot fix it; what the car does without the lift (not traced) is the thing to look at. (7) **Slowing the flick approach with the table** (−5 km/h at 2,325–2,385 m) gains 0.05 s at 2,500–2,600 m and loses 0.02 s before it, but the flick margin goes to 0.886 / 0.966: as in v1.04 and v1.05, a slower approach makes the arc entry worse. The flick tail is now the largest (30 runs, 0.030 s). (8) With the bends faster the car uses less of the inside (apexes −0.04 to −0.39 instead of −0.22 to −0.47): the per-bend line (v1.00: out-then-in gained at 1,528, 1,931 and 2,988 m) and the exits of 1,528 m and 2,700 m (+0.05 and +0.02 s given back after the bend) are the next table-driven candidates, together with `tc_slip_straight` 8 (not re-measured on this car). (9) Method: several rows can be screened in one 70-run config and read per section (the sections of far-apart bends barely interact: the per-bend figures from combined and composed trials agree within ~0.01 s, except 1,931 m at +14: −0.096 to −0.127). (10) Concern for the orchestrator: the table is tuned to this track, this car and this plan; any later change that moves a braking point by ~20 m (e.g. `brake_max_hi`, `brake_margin`) should re-check the row ends. The `brake_margin` ±2 and `brake_decel` +0.5 runs of the suites are part of the 70 and all gained. (11) Races used: about 1,820 (1,540 in valid trials, 210 lost to two scripting errors of mine, 70 final), above the 1,500 guide, in about 30 minutes of exploring; plus the version's run and rerun. |

## v1.07 — Line table (track memory): the side of the road to be on before a corner, keyed by `distFromStart` (new `line_table`, 2 rows: 1,931 m and the hairpin)

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v1.07 |
| **What changed** | `driver/snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v1.07`). One mechanism: **a line table**, the second use of track memory. New `line_table` (knob block, lines 651–661): rows of (from m, to m, trackPos aimed for, most steer). While `distFromStart` is inside a row **and the sensors have not detected a bend** (no side held from the look-ahead bearing), the car is pulled toward the row's trackPos with `line_gain`, by at most the row's steer; the row replaces the sensor corner set-up's pull on that stretch (code lines 729–741, directly after the set-up). Rows: **(1700, 1890, −0.7, 0.3)** = the approach to the 1,931 m left-hander from the right; **(3020, 3240, −0.85, 0.3)** = the approach to the hairpin (left) from the right. Bend detection, the turn-in, the line through the bend, the braking plan, ABS and traction control are unchanged and still react to the live sensors: the table only says which side to be on before a corner the sensors show too late. An empty table drives as v1.06. Line mapping from v1.06: old 651–717 +11, old ≥ 718 +24. No knob value changed; the corner table of v1.06 is unchanged. |

| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — 0 damage, 0 of 30 off, shifted bases 0 of 40 off, all-30 73.035 (v1.06 73.191), paired gain over all 70 runs −0.156 s at 15 standard errors with 67 of 70 runs faster; the 1,931 m exit and the hairpin exit both moved away from the edge (0.659 → 0.493, 0.770 → 0.667); one shifted run reads 0.852 at the flick (0.818 before), by knock-on, not by design. |
| **Learned** | (1) **Where the car is before the corner is worth 0.16 s at two corners, and the sensors could not have put it there**: the set-up starts when the road's end shows its slant (60–70 m before the braking point: ~1,780 m and ~3,115 m) and at the hairpin the car first has to cross from the left. The table starts the move 80–95 m earlier and holds it to the turn-in. (2) **v1.00's finding that a wide entry alone is slower at every medium bend no longer holds at 1,931 m** (−0.12 to −0.14 s; it was +0.111 s on v0.96's suite 1): with the corner table's +17 km/h the car was driving through the middle of the road there, and from the right it reaches the inside (+0.17) and carries 7 km/h more. It still holds at 446 m (+0.09 s), 770 m (+0.04), 1,528 m (+0.17) and 2,988 m (+0.15); 1,042 m is neutral (the car is already at +0.43 there). (3) **The trail-in is now wrong**: a soft pull to the inside before the detection halves the 1,931 m gain and loses 0.27 s alone; v1.00 needed it when the plan had no headroom. The sensor turn-in is the right one once the car is in the right place. (4) The useful target is about as far as the pull gets: with the cap at 0.3 the car reaches about −0.5 before 1,931 m and −0.57 before the hairpin; a target of −0.85 at 1,931 m is slower, −0.85 at the hairpin is 0.008 s better than −0.7 (why the two differ was not traced). (5) **Row ends matter by about 0.03 s per 15–20 m** (1,931 m: 1,870 / 1,890 / 1,905 m), far less than for the corner table. (6) The hairpin gain is small (−0.037 s): the car comes from the right and the bend is detected ~30 m earlier, but it still slides at full lock to 57 km/h; its exit is 0.10 further from the edge, which a faster hairpin row does not convert (row at 0: 0.999 of the edge). The hairpin needs a different idea (the arrival speed at the turn-in, not the side of the road). (7) **Open, measured:** the 2,700 m wide row (−0.025 s on top, 1.8 SE) and the 1,931 m corner-table row at +20 on the new line; both together with this version −0.212 s against v1.06 with 0 off, i.e. about −0.056 s more. (8) **Open, not measured:** individual row ends of the corner table; its offsets re-tuned on the new line (the 1,931 m exit now has half of the half-width free); line rows that act inside the bend (446 m, the flick); `tc_slip_straight` 8. (9) The largest tail is still the flick exit, 2,500–2,600 m (27 runs, 0.028 s). (10) Method: line rows of far-apart corners can be screened in one 70-run config and read per section, but not rows of neighbouring corners: 770 m read −0.014 s with the 446 m row before it and +0.042 s alone, and the 2,988 m row cost 0.06 s at the hairpin. (11) Races used: about 1,540 (22 configs of 70, one of them a repeat after a scripting error of mine that wrote the first trials to one folder), in about 30 minutes of exploring; plus the version's run and reruns. |

Tail table of the 70: 2,500–2,600 m 28 runs 0.024 s (was 27, 0.028), 200–300 m 14 runs 0.018 (unchanged), 500–600 m 0.013 (unchanged), 1,100–1,200 m 0.009, 1,500–1,600 m 7 runs 0.007 (was 17, 0.014), 1,000–1,100 m 2 runs 0.001 (was 10, 0.011); the mean lap is 1.11 s above the sum of per-section minima (was 1.19). `tools/patterns.py` (v1.07 → v1.08, single lap): 42 → 46 reversals, 0 → 1 episode (0.3 s at 1,605–1,619 m, 185 km/h: the sensor set-up's side flipping on the exit of the 1,528 m bend, steering amplitude 0.05; not looked into), 99 → 89 target flips, 92 → 86 plan jumps, 30 → 30 brake applications of which 19 → 18 short and 12 → 12 re-applied within 10 steps, 43 → 43 shifts with 3 → 3 undone within 1 s, TC 14.1 → 13.9 s. `tools/width.py` (entry / slowest point / exit; slowest speed): 447 m unchanged; 769 m +0.21 / −0.16 / +0.30 at 119 km/h → 779 m +0.20 / −0.17 / +0.28 at 120 (exit 179 → 187 km/h); 1,046 m +0.35 / −0.39 / +0.23 at 159 → 1,047 m +0.34 / −0.42 / +0.24 at 162; 1,517 m +0.11 / −0.24 / +0.17 at 141 → 1,526 m +0.10 / −0.35 / +0.12 at 144; 1,936 m +0.49 / −0.17 / +0.03 at 132 → 1,935 m +0.49 / −0.13 / +0.03 at 133; 2,990 m unchanged; **hairpin 57 → 70 km/h** (the tool reads its row as L +0.56 / +0.18 / −0.10 instead of R −0.56 / −0.45 / −0.37: the car is on the right 80 m before as in v1.07 and at −0.18 instead of −0.45 at its slowest point; it still does not reach the inside half); the flick row is read at 2,473 m, 65 km/h (was 2,458 m, 67 km/h) and the 2,731 m row is listed again (where the tool finds the slowest point). |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — 0 damage, 0 of 30 off, shifted bases 0 of 40 off, all-30 72.840 (v1.07 73.035), paired gain over all 70 runs −0.249 s at 14 standard errors with 68 of 70 runs faster, the gain inside the table's five zones, best single lap 1:12.75. Margin: suite max 0.796 → 0.783, shifted max 0.852 → 0.848, hairpin exit 0.667 → 0.623: none spent. |
| **Learned** | (1) **The false-start bend detections are not a useful delayed turn-in once the table can say where the turn-in belongs**: batch 11's conclusion held for rules acting at every bend; with one point per bend, skipping the false start is worth 0.03–0.07 s at each of 770, 1,042, 1,528 and 1,931 m and, with a slower approach, 0.04 s at the hairpin. (2) **The turn-in point has a cliff on the early side.** A row ending 3–6 m too early is 0.06–0.25 s slower than no row at all (1,042 m: 977 m +0.247 s; 1,528 m: 1,458 m +0.111; 1,931 m: 1,885 m +0.063); the best end is flat over about 3 m (1,461–1,464 m, 1,888–1,891 m, 3,236–3,238 m) and 6–12 m later is 0.08–0.18 s slower, 20 m later 0.2–0.65 s. The 770 m row is the narrowest (716 m −0.057 s; 712 m and 719 m neutral; 722 m +0.084): measured twice, but the first row to re-check if anything upstream changes. **Any change that moves a braking point or an approach speed by a few metres must re-check these ends**, as for the corner table. (3) **The braking plan is not conservative** (factor 1.15: 13–15 of 70 off and +0.3 to +0.45 s per bend after −0.05 to −0.10 s in the braking zone; a higher `brake_gain` in the zones is slower too): below ~180 km/h the car slows at 23–26 m/s² under the ABS cut with the wheel turned, whatever it does at 250 km/h at full pedal. (4) **Hairpin: the car now turns in once, from the right, and passes at 70 instead of 57 km/h, for 0.04 s**: what it gains at the slowest point it pays on the slower approach (3,240 m trial: 0.2 s behind at 3,255 m, level again at 3,285 m). More plan speed inside the hairpin runs wide (+12: 0.998 of the edge; +18: off): with \|steer\| 0.46–0.57 the car follows the road's direction (the heading term) and never reaches the inside half. The hairpin's remaining time is in its path, which needs a table-driven target inside the bend, not a speed. (5) Rows before the flick change the flick by chance (a trial with rows at 770, 1,528 and 2,988 m read 0.894 at the flick on one shifted run; the committed set reads 0.848, the base 0.852): re-check the flick maximum on the final set, as done here. (6) Method: turn-in points of far-apart bends can be scanned in one 70-run config and read per zone (the per-bend figures from scans and composed trials agree within 0.005 s; 1,042 m at 982 m: −0.031 / −0.038). (7) **Open, measured:** 1,042 m at 980 m (0.025 s more, 3 m from the cliff); 2,988 m at 2,929 m (−0.016 s). **Open, not measured:** turn-in points for the flick's two arcs and 446 m; a row that brings a turn-in forward; a line target inside the hairpin; the corner table re-tuned on the new turn-ins (incl. the 1,931 m row at +20) and the 2,700 m line row from v1.07. (8) Races used: about 1,640 (above the 1,500 guide: 23 configs of 70 and 23 four-run screens), in about 35 minutes of exploring; plus the version's run and rerun. |

Margin: the 1,931 m exit goes from 0.503 to 0.680 of the edge by design (the flick, the only margin place, reads 0.807 against 0.848 over the 70 runs; suite max 0.783 → 0.807 by knock-on); the cliffs are +35 km/h (3 of 70 off; chosen +29) and a row end at 1,885 m (0.969 of the edge; chosen 1,888 m). |
| **Learned** | (1) **A corner-table offset and its turn-in point have to be tuned together**: at the old turn-in (1,891 m) the row gains 0.03 / 0.05 / 0.07 / 0.08 s at +20 / +23 / +26 / +29; with the turn-in 3 m earlier 0.09 / 0.11 at +26 / +29 and the exit is *less* wide (0.618 against 0.639; 0.672 against 0.707): a faster arrival wants the earlier turn-in. (2) **The turn-in cliff does not move with the arrival speed** (row end 1,885 m is the cliff at +17, +26, +29 and +32: the false start under braking is back) but what it costs does: 0.06 s at +17, 0.86 of the edge at +26, 0.97 at +29, 13 of 70 off at +32. Any later change that makes the 1,931 m approach faster or its braking later must re-check 1,888 m. (3) **The 1,931 m bend is alone in this**: on the same test 1,042 m, 1,528 m, 2,700 m and 2,988 m are flat or slower at every offset and turn-in shift tried (best −0.015 s at 1,528 m), and more speed at 2,700 m is paid at the 2,988 m row (+0.105 s). The sensor plan was 29 km/h too slow only where v1.07 had put the car on the right before the bend; the rows of the other bends are at their optimum on the present line. (4) **Hairpin: an inside target is worth 0.02 s, not 0.1–0.3 s.** At the plan's 79–84 km/h the fronts are saturated: a pull of up to 0.5 steer moves the slowest point from −0.17 to −0.10 and no further; ended at 3,270 m it gains 0.022 s over the 70 runs (4.9 SE), held to 3,285 m nothing. Reaching the inside half needs a slower or earlier turn-in, which v1.08 scanned (3,232–3,240 m, row −7 / −9). (5) **Flick: slower into the left arc is not faster out** (−4 / −8 km/h over 2,395–2,470 m: +0.11 / +0.20 s for 0.05 of margin on the suites and none on the shifted bases): a plain trade, and at 446 m the split row is flat. (6) **Traction control by zone is real and free**: +3 m/s of straight-line limit only in 2,495–2,620 m gains 0.033 s at 3.8 standard errors with every maximum unchanged; the whole of `tc_slip_straight` 8's gain is that one exit. Ready-made for the next version (a traction table: rows of from m, to m, extra m/s). (7) Method: with one config per 36 s, a bend's offset and turn-in can be scanned as a grid in a few minutes; far-apart bends share a config and are read per zone. (8) **Open, measured:** traction table at the flick exit (−0.033 s); an in-bend line row at the hairpin (3,240–3,270 m toward +0.7, gain 1.0, cap 0.5: −0.022 s); 1,042 m turn end 980 m (−0.011 s), 2,988 m turn row to 2,929 m (−0.010 s); 1,528 m +5 with turn end 1,461 m (−0.015 s). **Open, not measured:** a line row before 2,700 m (−0.025 s on v1.07), a line row that puts the car on the outside before 1,528 m / 2,988 m *together with* a higher corner row and an earlier turn-in (what made 1,931 m work; the wide rows alone were slower on v1.06), turn rows for the flick's arcs and 446 m, traction rows at other exits (hairpin, 446 m: `tc_slip_straight` 8 everywhere showed nothing there). (9) Races used: about 1,850 (above the 1,500 guide: 24 configs of 70, one 28-run screen, base and final), in about 21 minutes of exploring. |

| **Learned** | (1) **The 1,931 m combination transfers to 1,528 m and 2,988 m, but only as a combination.** The line row alone is worth 0.01 s at 1,528 m and costs 0.42 s at 2,988 m; with the turn-in point moved 6 m earlier and +6 / +3 km/h on the corner row the two bends give 0.083 and 0.074 s. v1.06–v1.07's 'wide rows are slower there' and v1.09's 'the other bends' rows are at their optimum' held for one table at a time. (2) **A car on the outside wants its turn-in earlier, and the turn table's early-side cliff moves with the line**: from the centre the 1,528 m row ending 1,458 m was 0.11 s slower than no row (v1.08); from −0.45 it is the optimum and the slow side is at 1,452 m. Turn-in points are a property of the line, not of the track. (3) **A wide entry needs the false start removed first**: at 2,988 m the line row without a turn row is 0.42 s slower (a steer step of 0.2 at 2,919 m from +0.43 under braking), with one it gains. This may also be part of why v1.00 found a wide entry alone slower at every medium bend (not re-tested). (4) **It does not transfer to 1,042 m** (already entered at +0.34; every combination tried is flat or slower) **and at 770 m it is small and mixed** (−0.025 s at best, one config). (5) **Flick approach:** keeping the bend detection off through the kink (2,356–2,424 m) is worth 0.025 s at best for 0.06 of flick margin, and holding the car on the right before the left arc is 0.07–0.2 s slower (4-run screen only): on these trials the left arc does not gain from an outside entry. (6) The 1,931 m rows (+29, 1,888 m) are still at their optimum on the new approach, and its early-side cliff costs more now (1,885 m: 0.988 of the edge on one run, was 0.969). (7) `tools/patterns.py` prints a negative TC time on this run's log: the tool needs a look. (8) **Open, measured:** traction table at the flick exit (−0.033 s on v1.08, not re-measured); hairpin in-bend row (−0.022 s on v1.08); 2,988 m turn end 2,923–2,924 m (0.01 s more, nearer the slow side); 770 m line row + turn end 713 m + 5 km/h (−0.025 s, one config); flick no-bend row 2,356–2,424 m (−0.025 s on four runs, spends flick margin). **Open, not measured:** the same combination at 446 m (no false start there, so its turn row would fix the turn-in point) and at 2,700 m; the hairpin's line row re-tuned on the new 2,988 m exit; rows inside the flick (a line target between the two arcs, a turn-in for the right arc before 2,484 m); the 2 → 3 upshift over the crest at 2,484 m. (9) Races used: about 2,130 (above the 1,500 guide again: base, one 32-run screen, 25 trial configs of 70, the final 70, three re-check configs), in about 23 minutes of exploring. |
